{"batchcomplete":true,"query":{"pages":[{"pageid":48625599,"ns":0,"title":"UFC 200","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{short description|UFC mixed martial arts event in 2016}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=UFC 200: Tate vs Nunes\n|image=TateNunes_UFC200boutannouncement.jpg\n|image_size=215px\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date= {{start date|2016|7|9}}\n|venue= [[T-Mobile Arena]] \n|city= [[Paradise, Nevada]]\n|attendance=18,202{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/07/ufc-200-draws-announced-18202-fans-for-10-7-million-live-gate-at-new-t-mobile-arena|title=UFC 200 draws announced 18,202 fans for $10.7 million live gate at new T-Mobile Arena|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-07-10|access-date=2016-07-10}}\n|gate= $10,700,000\n|buyrate=1,009,000{{Cite web|url=http://members.f4wonline.com/wrestling-observer-newsletter/november-28-2016-wrestling-observer-newsletter-wwe-survivor-series|title=November 28, 2016 Wrestling Observer Newsletter|last=Meltzer|first=Dave|website=f4wonline.com|access-date=2017-01-08|url-access=subscription }}{{cite web | url=https://www.reddit.com/r/MMA/comments/5et6kg/meltzers_updated_ppv_info_ufc_196_mcgregordiaz_1/ | title=R/MMA - Meltzer's updated PPV info: UFC 196 (McGregor/Diaz 1) did 1,317,000 buys. UFC 200 did 1,009,000. No final estimate yet for UFC 202 (McGregor/Diaz 2) but it did break the all time record. UFC 205 is trending slightly bigger than 196 but probably short of 202 | date=25 November 2016 }}\n|previousevent=[[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Joanna vs. Team Cláudia#The Ultimate Fighter 23 Finale|The Ultimate Fighter: Team Joanna vs. Team Cláudia Finale]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC Fight Night: McDonald vs. Lineker]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 200: Tate vs. Nunes''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on July 9, 2016, at the [[T-Mobile Arena]] on the [[Las Vegas Strip]] in [[Paradise, Nevada]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/9/4/9263609/ufc-200-set-for-july-9-2016-at-new-las-vegas-arena|title=UFC 200 set for July 9, 2016 at new Las Vegas Arena|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2015-09-04|access-date=2015-09-04}} \n\nIt was the final UFC event under the ownership of [[Frank Fertitta III|Frank]] and [[Lorenzo Fertitta]], which began in January 2001. [[Zuffa]] subsequently announced its sale to a group led by [[WME-IMG]], an American [[talent agency]] with offices in [[Beverly Hills]], which included [[Silver Lake Partners]], [[Kohlberg Kravis Roberts]], and [[Michael Dell|MSD Partners]].{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/16970360|title=Dana White on $4 billion UFC sale: 'Sport is going to the next level'|publisher=espn.com.au|author=Darren Rovell and Brett Okamoto|date=2016-07-11|access-date=2016-07-11}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the first that the organization has hosted at [[T-Mobile Arena]], which opened in April 2016. It took place during the UFC's annual International Fight Week and marked the second time the UFC hosted three events in consecutive days.\n\n===Main event changes: McGregor is pulled; Jones tests positive; Tate-Nunes headline===\nA welterweight rematch between [[The Ultimate Fighter 5]] winner and former lightweight title challenger [[Nate Diaz]] and the then current [[UFC Featherweight Championship|UFC Featherweight Champion]] [[Conor McGregor]] was originally expected to headline the event.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/3/18/11261716/conor-mcgregor-vs-nate-diaz-rematch-in-the-works-for-ufc-200|title=Conor McGregor vs. Nate Diaz rematch in the works for UFC 200|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2016-03-18|access-date=2016-03-18}}{{cite news|url=http://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/ufc-conor-mcgregor-nate-diaz-frankie-edgar-jose-aldo-ufc-200-interim-title-033016|title=McGregor vs. Diaz 2, Aldo vs. Edgar for interim title official for UFC 200|work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2016-03-30|access-date=2016-03-30}} The pairing previously met earlier in the year at [[UFC 196]], where Diaz defeated McGregor via rear-naked choke in the second round.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/03/ufc-196-results-nate-diaz-shocks-conor-mcgregor-with-second-round-choke|title=UFC 196 results: Nate Diaz shocks Conor McGregor with second-round choke|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2016-03-06|access-date=2016-03-06}} McGregor was expected to challenge [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Rafael dos Anjos]], but dos Anjos pulled out due to a [[broken foot]] only 11 days before the event. Diaz eventually replaced him and the bout was shifted to the welterweight division.{{cite news|url=http://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/ufc-196-conor-mcgregor-nate-diaz-new-main-event-022316|title=Nate Diaz gets the call, faces Conor McGregor in new UFC 196 main event|work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2016-02-23|access-date=2016-03-29}}\n\nOn April 19, after McGregor [[Twitter|tweeted]] a supposed retirement, the UFC announced that he was pulled from the event and a replacement for him was being sought. UFC President [[Dana White]] clarified that McGregor's removal was related to his refusal to come to a press conference that week, because \"he was in [[Iceland]] training and didn't want to ruin his preparation for the fight\".{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/4/19/11464766/dana-white-conor-mcgregor-pulled-from-ufc-200-because-he-wouldnt|title=Dana White: Conor McGregor pulled from UFC 200, because he wouldn't attend press conference|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-04-19|access-date=2016-04-19}} McGregor released a statement two days later, claiming he was not retired and that he requested the UFC to allow him to focus more on the fight preparation this time, as he felt he lost his focus during the media obligations for the previous fight. He then stated that he was ready for the event and would come for a scheduled [[New York City|New York]] press conference, but if that's not enough, he \"doesn't know what to say\".{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/4/21/11478120/conor-mcgregor-issues-statement-after-being-pulled-from-ufc-200|title=Conor McGregor issues statement, says he's not retired after all|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Bryan Tucker|date=2016-04-21|access-date=2016-04-21}} A report later claimed that the UFC decided to cancel the fight indeed, in what was rumored to be a $10 million paycheck for McGregor.{{cite web|url=https://www.tmz.com/2016/04/21/conor-mcgregor-10-million-ufc-officially-calls-off-fight/|title=Conor McGregor takes $10 mil hit. UFC officially calls off fight|publisher=tmz.com|author=Staff|date=2016-04-21|access-date=2016-04-21}}\n\n[[File:T-Mobile Arena (31534778122).jpg|thumb|left|250px|[[T-Mobile Arena (Las Vegas)|T-Mobile Arena]] hosted the much anticipated event, which was the 363rd overall in UFC history]]\nOn April 27, the UFC officially announced the new headliner as a [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] unification rematch between [[Daniel Cormier]] and [[Jon Jones]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/04/daniel-cormier-vs-jon-jones-title-rematch-headlines-and-saves-ufc-200|title=Daniel Cormier vs. Jon Jones title rematch headlines (and saves?) UFC 200|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Dann Stupp|date=2016-04-27|access-date=2016-04-27}} The pairing met previously at [[UFC 182]] in January 2015 with Jones defending his title via unanimous decision.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/01/ufc-182-results-jon-jones-tested-but-defends-title-against-daniel-cormier|title=UFC 182 results: Jon Jones tested, but defends title against Daniel Cormier|author=Steven Marrocco|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2015-01-04|access-date=2016-04-27}} Subsequent to that victory, Jones was stripped of the title and suspended indefinitely from the UFC in connection with a [[Jon Jones#Hit-and-run conviction|hit-and-run incident]] that he was involved in.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/4/28/8512255/jon-jones-stripped-of-title-daniel-cormier-vs-anthony-johnson-for|title=Jon Jones stripped of title, Daniel Cormier vs. Anthony Johnson for belt headlines UFC 187|author=Marc Raimondi|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2015-04-29|access-date=2016-04-27}} Cormier replaced him and went on to defeat [[Anthony Johnson (fighter)|Anthony Johnson]] at [[UFC 187]] to win the vacant title.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/05/ufc-187-results-daniel-cormier-wears-down-anthony-johnson-for-third-round-tap|title=UFC 187 results: Daniel Cormier wears down Anthony Johnson for third-round tap|author=Steven Marrocco|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2015-05-24|access-date=2016-04-27}} Their rematch was originally expected to take place at [[UFC 197]], but Cormier pulled out three weeks before the event due to injury and was replaced by [[Ovince Saint Preux]], in what became an interim title bout.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/4/2/11353572/jon-jones-vs-ovince-saint-preux-set-for-ufc-197-with-daniel-cormier|title=Jon Jones vs. Ovince Saint Preux set for UFC 197 with Daniel Cormier out|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-04-02|access-date=2016-04-27}} Jones went on to defeat Saint Preux by unanimous decision and won the interim title.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2016/4/24/11496188/ufc-197-results-jon-jones-ovince-st-preux|title=UFC 197 results: rusty Jon Jones shuts down Ovince St. Preux, wins interim light heavyweight title|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Thomas Myers|date=2016-04-24|access-date=2016-04-27}}\n\nThe event suffered another major hit only three days before it happened, as it was announced that Jones was pulled out by [[USADA]] due to a potential Anti-Doping Policy violation stemming from an out-of-competition sample collection on June 16. Cormier declared he would still fight if an opponent brought to him \"made sense\".{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/16844105/ufc-200-jon-jones-faces-anti-doping-violation-removed-card|title=Jon Jones removed from UFC 200 for possible doping violation|publisher=[[ESPN]]|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2016-07-06|access-date=2016-07-06}} A day later Jones apologized for the incident, but denied knowingly taking any illegal substance. He and his manager also declined to specify the substance that resulted in the failed test.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/7/7/12119378/emotional-jon-jones-denies-taking-any-illegal-substance-apologizes-to|title=Emotional Jon Jones denies taking any illegal substance, apologizes to Daniel Cormier|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|date=2016-07-07|access-date=2016-07-07}} His \"B\" sample also came positive for the same substances and Jones' faced a potential two-year suspension.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/07/usada-testing-of-jon-jones-b-sample-confirms-initial-findings|title=USADA: Testing of Jon Jones' 'B' sample confirms initial findings|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-07-08|access-date=2016-07-08}}\n\nDuring the [[UFC Fight Night: dos Anjos vs. Alvarez]] broadcast, it was announced that former [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] [[Anderson Silva]] would replace Jones on less than two days' notice and face Cormier in a three-round non-title light heavyweight bout.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/7/7/12126438/anderson-silva-steps-in-to-face-daniel-cormier-in-non-title-bout-at|title=Anderson Silva steps in to face Daniel Cormier in non-title bout at UFC 200 on two days notice|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-07-07|access-date=2016-07-07}}\n\nDue to those major changes, the already scheduled [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]] bout between then champion [[Miesha Tate]] and top contender [[Amanda Nunes]] was revealed as the new main event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/04/amanda-nunes-challenges-miesha-tate-for-womens-bantamweight-title-at-ufc-200|title=Amanda Nunes challenges Miesha Tate for women's bantamweight title at UFC 200|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-04-06|access-date=2016-04-06}}\n\n===Main card: a former champion returns; an interim champion is crowned===\n[[File:Brock Lesnar WWE Champion 2014.jpg|thumb|200px|Former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight Champion]] [[Brock Lesnar]] had a [[leave of absence]] from the [[WWE]] to compete at this event, his first fight since [[UFC 141]] in December 2011]]\n\nOn June 4, a few hours before the [[UFC 199]] event, MMAFighting.com reporter [[Ariel Helwani]] broke the news that former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight Champion]] [[Brock Lesnar]] was close to finalizing a deal to return at this event, despite being active in the [[WWE]]. The UFC confirmed the report, via a teaser video clip on the UFC 199 main card broadcast.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/06/brock-lesnar-returns-to-the-octagon-in-ufc-200-co-main-event|title=Brock Lesnar returns to the octagon in UFC 200 co-main event|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Brent Brookhouse|date=2016-06-04|access-date=2016-06-04}} However, Helwani's news scoop earned him a prompt physical ejection from the event venue and a lifetime ban from covering future UFC live events.{{cite web|url=https://www.businessinsider.com/ufc-reporter-ariel-helwani-banned-by-ufc-for-life-2016-6|title=Reporter says he has been banned from UFC for life...|publisher=[[Business Insider]]|author=Emmett Knowlton|date=2016-06-07|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20190225223825/https://www.businessinsider.com.au/ufc-reporter-ariel-helwani-banned-by-ufc-for-life-2016-6 |access-date=2016-07-12|url-status=live|archive-date=2019-02-25 }} The organization faced a major backlash from the media community as well as fans,{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2016/6/6/11870434/ufc-199-media-condemns-ufc-treatment-ariel-helwani-brock-lesnar-return-report-mma-news|title=Media condemns UFC's treatment of Ariel Helwani|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Zane Simon|date=2016-06-06|access-date=2016-07-12}} before reinstating Helwani's media credentials. They also stated that it was their belief \"recurring tactics used\" by Helwani \"extended beyond the purpose of journalism\", a statement that MMAFighting.com disagreed with.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/6/7/11874290/mma-fighting-statement-on-credential-bans-lifted-by-ufc|title=MMA Fighting statement on credential bans lifted by UFC|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Bryan Tucker|date=2016-06-07|access-date=2016-07-12}}\n\nLesnar faced the [[K-1 World Grand Prix 2001 Final|2001 K-1 World Grand Prix]] winner and former interim title contender [[Mark Hunt]].{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/15995312|title=Brock Lesnar to face Mark Hunt in UFC one-off|publisher=[[ESPN]]|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2016-06-06|access-date=2016-06-06}} Due to Jones' removal from the card, this bout was briefly promoted as the new main event. However, after the Tate-Nunes bout was announced as the new headliner, Lesnar-Hunt was once again confirmed as the co-main event. Lesnar also headlined [[UFC 100]] and with Jones' absence, he and [[Jim Miller (fighter)|Jim Miller]] remained as the only two fighters to compete at both milestone events.\n\nDue to McGregor's experiments outside of his division, an interim [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] bout between former champion [[José Aldo]] and former lightweight champion [[Frankie Edgar]] took place at this event. It was a rematch, as Aldo previously defended his title against Edgar at [[UFC 156]] in 2013 via unanimous decision.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2013/02/ufc-156-resultsphotos-champ-jose-aldo-vs-frankie-edgar|title=UFC 156 results/photos: Jose Aldo retains title by outstriking Frankie Edgar|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Matt Erickson|date=2013-02-03|access-date=2016-03-30}} Aldo once again won by unanimous decision.{{Cite web |last=Doyle |first=Dave |date=2016-07-09 |title=Aldo decision Edgar, claims interim FW belt |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2016/7/9/12138318/ufc-200-results-jose-aldo-outpoints-frankie-edgar-claims-interim |access-date=2022-06-18 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}}\n\nThe main card opened with a heavyweight bout between former two-time champion [[Cain Velasquez]] and [[Travis Browne]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/03/cain-velasquez-vs-travis-browne-targeted-for-ufc-200|title=Cain Velasquez vs. Travis Browne targeted for UFC 200|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2016-03-16|access-date=2016-03-16}}\n\n===Stacked under-card===\nThe featured bout of the preliminary card was a women's bantamweight contest between former title challenger [[Cat Zingano]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Rousey vs. Team Tate]] winner [[Julianna Peña]].{{cite web|url=http://themmacorner.com/2016/03/31/cat-zingano-vs-julianna-pena-sage-northcutt-vs-enrique-marin-added-to-ufc-200/|title=Cat Zingano vs. Julianna Peña added to UFC 200|publisher=themmacorner.com|author=Jose Youngs|date=2016-03-31|access-date=2016-03-31}} A few other bouts were also part of the [[Fox Sports 1]] televised prelims: \n*a welterweight bout between former [[UFC Welterweight Championship|UFC Welterweight Champion]] [[Johny Hendricks]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Jones vs. Team Sonnen]] winner [[Kelvin Gastelum]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/3/29/11324274/johny-hendricks-vs-kelvin-gastelum-in-the-works-for-ufc-200|title=Johny Hendricks vs. Kelvin Gastelum in the works for UFC 200|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2016-03-29|access-date=2016-03-29}}\n*a bantamweight rematch between former [[UFC Bantamweight Championship|UFC Bantamweight Champion]] [[T.J. Dillashaw]] and [[Raphael Assunção]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Dillashaw-Assuncao-II-Official-for-UFC-200|title=Dillashaw-Assunção II official for UFC 200|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2016-05-10|access-date=2016-05-10}} The pairing first met at [[UFC Fight Night: Maia vs. Shields]] in October 2013, when Assunção won a close bout via split decision.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2013/10/ufc-fight-night-29-results-photos-raphael-assuncao-vs-t-j-dillashaw|title=UFC Fight Night 29 results, photos: Raphael Assunção gets past T.J. Dillashaw|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Matt Erickson|date=2013-10-09|access-date=2016-05-10}}\n*Opening that portion of the event in the lightweight division was a bout between [[Sage Northcutt]] and Enrique Marín.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/03/sage-northcutt-meets-enrique-marin-at-stacked-ufc-200|title=Sage Northcutt meets Enrique Marin at stacked UFC 200 (Updated)|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-03-31|access-date=2016-03-31}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Joe Lauzon]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter 1]] winner and former lightweight title challenger [[Diego Sanchez]] was originally booked for [[UFC 180]]. However, the bout was cancelled due to both fighters being injured.{{cite web|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Injuries-Result-in-Cancellation-of-Diego-SanchezJoe-Lauzon-Bout-at-UFC-180-76145|title=Injuries result in cancellation of Diego Sanchez-Joe Lauzon bout at UFC 180|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2014-10-23|access-date=2016-03-29}} The fight was later rescheduled for this event and headlined the [[UFC Fight Pass]] preliminary card.{{cite web|url=http://vendettafighter.com/diego-sanchez-vs-joe-lauzon-in-the-works-ufc-200/|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160321111609/http://vendettafighter.com/diego-sanchez-vs-joe-lauzon-in-the-works-ufc-200/|url-status=usurped|archive-date=March 21, 2016|title=Diego Sanchez vs Joe Lauzon in the works UFC 200|publisher=vendettafighter.com|author=Jay Russell|date=2016-03-29|access-date=2016-03-29}}\n\nA middleweight bout between former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Light Heavyweight Championship|Strikeforce Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Gegard Mousasi]] and [[Derek Brunson]] was expected to take place at the event, but on June 19 it was announced that Brunson pulled out due to injury and was replaced by [[Thiago Santos (fighter)|Thiago Santos]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/03/verbal-agreements-in-place-for-derek-brunson-vs-gegard-mousasi-at-ufc-200|title=Verbal agreements in place for Derek Brunson vs. Gegard Mousasi at UFC 200|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-03-17|access-date=2016-03-17}}{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/6/19/11973368/gegard-mousasi-vs-thiago-santos-in-the-works-ufc-200|title=Gegard Mousasi vs. Thiago Santos in the works for UFC 200|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2016-06-19|access-date=2016-06-19}}\n\nFormer [[List of Pride champions#World Lightweight Championship|PRIDE Lightweight Champion]] [[Takanori Gomi]] and [[Jim Miller (fighter)|Jim Miller]] were chosen to open the event in a lightweight bout.\n\n===Weigh-ins===\nAt the weigh-ins, Hendricks missed weight by a quarter of a pound, weighing in at 171.25 lb. He was not given a second attempt to make the weight because the recently introduced early weigh-in procedures set a 10:00 a.m. cut-off time and Hendricks weighed in at the last possible moment. As a result, he was fined 20% of his fight purse, which went to Gastelum.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/07/ufc-200-live-and-official-weigh-in-results-2|title=UFC 200 weigh-in results: Hendricks misses weight as champ Tate narrowly makes deadline|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-06-08|access-date=2016-05-10}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main It does not include sponsor money and also does not include the UFC's traditional \"fight night\" bonuses.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/07/ufc-200-salaries-brock-lesnars-2-5-million-leads-disclosed-payroll-of-nearly-7-million|title=UFC 200 salaries: Brock Lesnar's $2.5 million leads disclosed payroll of nearly $7 million|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Matt Erickson|date=2016-07-11|access-date=2016-07-11}} \n* '''Amanda Nunes:''' $100,000 (no win bonus) def. Anderson Silva: $600,000\n* '''José Aldo:''' $500,000 (includes $100,000 win bonus) def. Frankie Edgar: $190,000\n* '''Cain Velasquez:''' $300,000 (no win bonus) def. Johny Hendricks: $80,000 ^\n* '''T.J. Dillashaw:''' $50,000 (includes $25,000 win bonus) def. Raphael Assunção: $42,000\n* '''Sage Northcutt:''' $100,000 (includes $50,000 win bonus) def. Enrique Marín: $13,000\n* '''Joe Lauzon:''' $108,000 (includes $54,000 win bonus) def. Diego Sanchez: $80,000\n* '''Gegard Mousasi:''' $110,000 (includes $35,000 win bonus) def. Takanori Gomi: $55,000\n\n^ ''Johny Hendricks was fined 20 percent of his purse ($20,000) for failing to make the required weight for his fight with Kelvin Gastelum. That money was issued to Gastelum, an NSAC official confirmed.''\n\n==Records set==\nThe event had a $10,700,000 gate, which broke the record for a mixed martial arts event in the United States. The final attendance was 18,202, a record for [[Nevada]], which had hosted 104 prior UFC events. The total disclosed payout for the event reached nearly $7 million at $6,979,000, believed to be the highest combined disclosed payday in UFC history. Lesnar's fight purse was also the highest, breaking [[Conor McGregor]]'s record from [[UFC 196]] by $1.5 million.{{cite web|url=http://www.foxsports.com.au/ufc/brock-lesnar-earns-highest-purse-in-history-for-ufc-200-appearance-beating-conor-mcgregors-record/news-story/5f12d35b3d215860f9a00399a5ea54a6|title=Brock Lesnar earns highest purse in history for UFC 200 appearance, beating Conor McGregor's record|publisher=foxsports.com.au|author=Damon Martin|date=2016-07-10|access-date=2016-07-10}}\n\n==Collaboration with Mike Judge==\nOn September 15, 2010, [[Yahoo Sports]] reported that UFC president [[Dana White]] was hanging out with ''[[Beavis and Butt-Head]]'' creator, [[Mike Judge]], of which they wrote \"White says the UFC and Judge will be working together\".{{cite news |last1=Cofield |first1=Steve |title=Dana White hanging with 'Beavis and Butthead' guy? |url=https://sports.yahoo.com/blogs/mma-cagewriter/dana-white-hanging-beavis-butthead-guy--mma.html |access-date=18 July 2022 |publisher=Yahoo! Sports |date=15 Sep 2010}} In a rare collaboration, Judge who is a huge fan of the UFC{{cite news |last1=Illuminati |first1=Chris |title=We Asked UFC President Dana White The Most Important Fight Question Ever — Who'd Win In A Brawl Between Beavis And Butthead? |url=https://brobible.com/sports/article/ufc-adult-swim/ |access-date=18 July 2022 |publisher=brobible |date=1 July 2016}} worked on a special project for UFC 200 creating an animated short to promote the event,{{cite news |last1=Golighty |first1=Justin |title=Creators of Beavis & Butthead and Ren & Stimpy Are Doing a UFC 200 Cartoon |url=https://middleeasy.com/mma-news/creators-beavis-butthead-ren-stimpy-ufc-200-cartoon/ |access-date=18 July 2022 |publisher=middleeasy |date=25 June 2016}} which also featured [[Hank Hill]] from ''[[King of the Hill]]'', another one of Judge's shows.{{cite news |title=See Animated Take on Epic UFC 200 Fight Between Daniel Cormier, Jon Jones |url=https://www.rollingstone.com/culture/culture-sports/see-animated-take-on-epic-ufc-200-fight-between-daniel-cormier-jon-jones-111601/ |access-date=18 July 2022 |publisher=Rolling Stone |date=29 June 2016}}{{cite news |last1=Lynch |first1=Jason |title=King of the Hill's Hank Returns to Promote UFC 200 in New Adult Swim Branded Spot |url=https://www.adweek.com/convergent-tv/king-hill-s-hank-returns-promote-ufc-200-new-adult-swim-branded-spot-172298/ |access-date=18 July 2022 |publisher=adweek |date=29 June 2016}}{{cite news |title=Hank Hill Has Been Talking Trash About Jon Jones' Skinny Legs |url=https://fightstate.com/hank-hill-has-been-talking-trash-about-jon-jones-skinny-legs/ |access-date=18 July 2022 |publisher=fightstate}}\n\n==Aftermath==\nOn July 15, it was announced that [[USADA]] informed Lesnar of a potential Anti-Doping Policy violation stemming from an out-of-competition sample collection on June 28 from the [[World Anti-Doping Agency|WADA]]-accredited UCLA Olympic Analytical Laboratory on the evening of July 14.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/07/usada-brock-lesnar-fails-out-of-competition-test-for-ufc-200|title=USADA: Brock Lesnar fails out-of-competition test for UFC 200|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2016-07-15|access-date=2016-07-15}} Four days later, USADA announced another failed test: this time in-competition. It was the same substance, which was not disclosed.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/07/usada-brock-lesnars-ufc-200-in-competition-test-also-tested-positive|title=USADA: Brock Lesnar's UFC 200 in-competition test also tested positive|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-07-19|access-date=2016-07-19}} On July 23, reports came that Lesnar tested positive for hydroxy-clomiphene, which was revealed days earlier as one of the substances responsible for fellow UFC competitor Jon Jones' test failure.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/7/23/12261944/brock-lesnar-tested-positive-for-anti-estrogen-lesnar-jon-jones-won-t|title=Brock Lesnar tested positive for anti-estrogen; Lesnar, Jon Jones won't face UFC fine|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-07-23|access-date=2016-07-23}} Lesnar was eventually suspended by the [[Nevada Athletic Commission|NSAC]] on December 15 for a period of one year, retroactive to the date of the event and his win was overturned to a no contest. He was also fined 10% of his purse. In early January 2017, USADA also suspended Lesnar for the same period, but this time retroactive to the moment he was provisionally suspended.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/01/former-ufc-champ-brock-lesnar-gets-1-year-suspension-from-usada|title=Former UFC champ Brock Lesnar gets 1-year suspension from USADA|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2017-01-04|access-date=2017-01-04}}\n\nOn July 18, the NSAC confirmed the substances for which Jones tested positive: [[clomifene|hydroxy-clomiphene]], an [[Antiestrogen|anti-estrogenic]] agent and [[letrozole]] metabolite, an [[aromatase inhibitor]]. A temporary suspension placed on Jones' Nevada fight license was subsequently extended by a unanimous vote, while a formal hearing was expected for September or October.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/07/nsac-ufc-interm-champ-jon-jones-positive-for-2-estrogen-blockers-receives-temporary-suspension|title=NSAC: UFC interim champ Jon Jones positive for 2 estrogen blockers, receives temporary suspension|date=18 July 2016 |publisher=mmajunkie.com}}\n\nOn November 7, USADA announced that Jones was suspended for a period of one year retroactive to the July 6 date on which he was provisionally suspended.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/11/jon-jones-gets-1-year-suspension-from-usada-for-doping-violation-stemming-from-ufc-200|title=Jon Jones gets 1-year suspension from USADA for doping violation stemming from UFC 200|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2016-11-07|access-date=2016-11-07}} Two days later, the UFC announced that Jones was stripped of the interim title.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/11/ufc-strips-jon-jones-of-interim-light-heavyweight-title-on-heels-of-one-year-suspension|title=UFC strips Jon Jones of interim light heavyweight title on heels of one-year suspension|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-11-09|access-date=2016-11-09}} The NSAC also suspended Jones for the same period as USADA.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/12/8/13892798/jon-jones-reaches-settlement-with-nevada-athletic-commission|title=Jon Jones reaches agreement with Nevada Athletic Commission, suspended one year|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2016-12-08|access-date=2016-12-08}}\n\n==See also==\n*[[List of UFC events]]\n*[[2016 in However, on 24 December, Mousasi was replaced by former [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] [[Anderson Silva]] in favour of a main event.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/12/24/10663950/anderson-silva-vs-michael-bisping-headlines-london-card-on-fight-pass|title=Anderson Silva vs. Michael Bisping headlines London card on Fight Pass|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|access-date=24 December 2015|date=24 December 2015}} Three days later, it was announced that Mousasi would face former title challenger [[Thales Leites]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/2015/12/gegard-mousasi-now-meets-thales-leites-at-ufc-fight-night-83-in-london|title=Gegard Mousasi now meets Thales Leites at UFC Fight Night 83 in London|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=27 December 2015|date=27 December 2015}}\n\n[[Jimi Manuwa]] was expected to face [[Nikita Krylov]] at this event. However 16 February, Sajewski pulled out due to an injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Thibault Gouti]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/02/with-sajewski-out-teemu-packalen-meets-thibault-gouti-at-ufc-fight-night-84-in-london|title=With Sajewski out, Teemu Packalen meets Thibault Gouti at UFC Fight Night 84 in London|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=16 February 2016|date=16 February 2016}}\n\nThe event tied [[UFC 169]] and [[UFC Fight Night: Machida vs. Mousasi]] for the most decisions at a single UFC event with 10.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2016/02/ufc-fight-night-84-post-fight-facts-bisping-makes-big-splash-in-ufc-record-books|title=UFC Fight Night 84 vs. Bisping]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Cowboy vs. Cowboy''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 83''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on February 21, 2016, at the [[Consol Energy Center]] in [[Pittsburgh, Pennsylvania]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-Sets-February-Date-in-Pittsburgh|title=UFCcgxggX sets February date in Pittsburgh|publisher=ufc.com|author=UFC Press Release|date=2015-12-03|access-date=2015-12-03}}{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/12/ufc-fight-night-82-set-for-pittsburghs-consol-energy-center-on-feb-21|title=UFC Fight Night 82 set for Pittsburgh's Consol Energy Center on Feb. 21|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-12-03|access-date=2015-12-03}}\n\n==Background==\nThis was the second event that the UFC hosted in [[Pittsburgh]], and first since [[UFC Live: Kongo vs. Barry]] in June 2011. It was originally expected to be headlined by a [[Welterweight (MMA)|welterweight]] bout between former [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] challenger [[Donald Cerrone]] and [[Tim Means]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/01/cowboy-cerrone-moves-to-welterweight-meets-tim-means-in-ufc-fight-night-82-main-event|title='Cowboy' Cerrone moves to 170, meets Tim Means in UFC Fight Night 82 main event|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Matt Erickson|date=2016-01-01|access-date=2016-01-02}} On February 3, it was announced that Means was informed of a potential [[USADA]] anti-doping policy violation stemming from an out-of-competition sample collection. Because there was insufficient time for a full review and proper promotion before the scheduled bout, Means was pulled from the fight by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/02/tim-means-pulled-from-ufc-fight-night-83-headliner-after-failed-usada-test|title=Tim Means pulled from UFC Fight Night 83 headliner after failed USADA test|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-02-03|access-date=2016-02-03}} The following day, [[Alex Oliveira]] was announced as his replacement.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/02/cowboy-vs-cowboy-targeted-as-new-ufc-fight-night-83-headliner|title='Cowboy' vs. 'Cowboy' set as new UFC Fight Night 83 headliner (Updated)|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-02-04|access-date=2016-02-04}}\n\n[[Sam Alvey]] was expected to face [[Daniel Sarafian]] at the event. However, Alvey pulled out of the bout in late December after sustaining a broken jaw and was replaced by [[Oluwale Bamgbose]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/12/report-sam-alvey-suffers-broken-jaw-out-of-ufc-fight-night-82-vs-daniel-sarafian|title=Sam Alvey suffers broken jaw, out of UFC Fight Night 82 vs. Daniel Sarafian|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-12-29|access-date=2015-12-29}}{{cite web|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Bamgbose-Replaces-Injured-Alvey-Meets-Sarafian-at-UFC-Fight-Night-Pittsburgh-98113|title=Oluwale Bamgbose replaces injured Sam Alvey, meets Daniel Sarafian at UFC Fight Night Pittsburgh|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2016-01-05|access-date=2016-01-05}}\n\n[[Bryan Barberena]] was briefly linked to a bout with Jonavin Webb at the event. However, on January 22, Barberena was pulled from that fight in favor of a matchup with [[Sage Northcutt]] on January 30 at [[UFC on Fox: Johnson vs. Bader]], replacing an injured [[Andrew Holbrook]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/01/andrew-holbrook-out-bryan-barberena-meets-sage-northcutt-at-ufc-on-fox-18|title=Andrew Holbrook out, Bryan Barberena meets Sage Northcutt at UFC on FOX 18|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2016-01-22|date=2016-01-22}} He was replaced by [[Nathan Coy]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Coy-Webb-Meet-in-the-Steel-City|title=Coy, Webb meet in the Steel City|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|access-date=2016-01-23|date=2016-01-23}}\n\n[[Sarah Moras]] was expected to face former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Bantamweight Championship|Invicta FC Bantamweight Champion]] [[Lauren Murphy]] at the event. However, Moras pulled out of the bout on February 12 citing injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/02/sarah-moras-out-at-ufc-fight-night-83-ufc-seeks-opponent-for-lauren-murphy|title=Sarah Moras out at UFC Fight Night 83, UFC seeks opponent for Lauren Murphy|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|access-date=2016-02-12|date=2016-02-12}} She was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Kelly Faszholz]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/02/lauren-murphy-meets-replacement-kelly-faszholz-on-sunday-at-ufc-fight-night-83|title=Lauren Murphy meets replacement Kelly Faszholz on Sunday at UFC Fight Night 83|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|access-date=2016-02-16|date=2016-02-16}}\n\nA [[Welterweight (MMA)|welterweight]] bout between [[Brandon Thatch]] and [[Siyar Bahadurzada]], originally slated for this event was rescheduled due to Thatch's inability to gain clearance for needed medication for the event. The bout will take place two weeks later at [[UFC 196]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/02/medication-clearance-issue-moves-siyar-bahadurzada-vs-brandon-thatch-to-ufc-196|title='Medication' clearance issue moves Siyar Bahadurzada vs. Brandon Thatch to UFC 196|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-02-12|access-date=2016-02-12}}\n\n[[John Lineker]] was expected to face [[Cody Garbrandt]] at the event. However, on February 15, Lineker pulled out of the fight due to [[dengue fever]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/02/john-lineker-out-of-ufc-fight-night-83-bout-vs-garbrandt-with-dengue-fever|title=John Lineker out of UFC Fight Night 83 bout vs. Garbrandt with dengue fever|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-02-15|access-date=2016-02-15}} He was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Augusto Mendes]].{{cite news|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Augusto-Mendes-to-Meet-Cody-Garbrandt-on-Short-Notice-at-UFC-Fight-Night-Pittsburgh-100289|title=Augusto Mendes to meet Cody Garbrandt on short notice at UFC Fight Night Pittsburgh|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2016-02-16|access-date=2016-02-16}} Mendes missed weight on his first attempt at the weigh ins, coming in at 142 lb. He was given additional time to make the weight limit for the weight class, but made no attempts to cut further. Instead, he was fined 20 percent of his fight purse, which went to Garbrandt and the commission.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/02/ufc-fight-night-83-live-and-official-weigh-in-results-2|title=UFC Fight Night 83 weigh-in results: Both 'Cowboys' make weight; one fighter heavy|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-02-20|access-date=2016-02-20}}\n\nAlso on the week leading up to the event, [[Trevor Smith (fighter)|Trevor Smith]] pulled out of his [[Middleweight (MMA)|middleweight]] bout against Leonardo Augusto Guimaraes due to a hand injury.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/02/trevor-smith-injured-out-of-ufc-fight-night-83-bout-with-leonardo-augusto-guimaraes|title=Trevor Smith injured, out of UFC Fight Night 83 bout with Leonardo Augusto Guimaraes|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-02-15|access-date=2016-02-15}} He was replaced by returning veteran [[Anthony Smith (fighter)|Anthony Smith]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/02/anthony-smith-returns-to-ufc-replaces-trevor-smith-at-sundays-ufc-fight-night-83|title=Anthony Smith returns to UFC, replaces Trevor Smith at Sunday's UFC Fight Night 83|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-02-16|access-date=2016-02-16}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent event in 2016}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=UFC 197: Jones vs. Saint Preux\n|image=DC vs. Jones 2.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date= {{start date|2016|04|23}}\n|venue= [[MGM Grand Garden Arena]]\n|city= [[Las Vegas, Nevada]]\n|attendance= 11,352 {{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/04/ufc-197-attendance-event-draws-11352-fans-for-2-3-million-live-gate-in-las-vegas|title=UFC 197 attendance: event draws 11,352 fans for $2.3 million live gate in Night: Overeem vs. Arlovski]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 197: Jones vs. Saint Preux''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on April 23, 2016, at [[MGM Grand Garden Arena]] in [[Las Vegas Valley|Las Vegas]], Nevada.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/02/ufc-197-set-for-april-23-in-vegas-with-cormier-jones-2-johnson-cejudo-title-fights|title=UFC 197 set for April 23 in Vegas with Cormier-Jones 2, Johnson-Cejudo title fights|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn |date=2016-02-06|access-date=2016-02-07}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event's number was initially planned for an event taking place on March 5, 2016 at [[HSBC Arena (Rio de Janeiro)|HSBC Arena]] in [[Rio de Janeiro]], [[Brazil]],{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/11/ufc-197-set-for-hsbc-arena-in-rio-de-janeiro-on-march-5|title=UFC 197 set for HSBC Arena in Rio de Janeiro on March 5|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2015-11-08|access-date=2016-01-08}} before being cancelled on December 29, 2015{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/12/29/10684682/ufc-cancels-event-in-rio-de-janeiro-due-to-economic-crisis|title=Anderson Silva: UFC cancels event in Rio de Janeiro|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2015-12-29|access-date=2016-01-08}} and moved to MGM Grand Garden Arena in Las Vegas, Nevada, to take place on February 6, 2016; the February event became [[UFC Fight Night: Hendricks vs. Thompson]] rather than a numbered pay-per-view after [[Vitor Belfort]] declined to fight [[Anderson Silva]],{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/1/13/10761276/ufc-official-confirms-vitor-belfort-turned-down-fight-with-anderson|title=UFC official confirms Vitor Belfort turned down fight with Anderson Silva at UFC 197|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2016-01-13|access-date=2016-01-13}} and the March show was renumbered as [[UFC 196]].\n\nThe UFC hoped to contest the event at [[Madison Square Garden]] in [[New York City]], in what would be the first event in the state of [[New York (state)|New York]] since the state's MMA ban.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2015/9/28/9410591/ufc-sues-state-new-york-books-ppv-event-april-23-msg-mma|title=UFC sues state of New York, books PPV event for April 23 in Madison Square Garden|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Jesse Holland|date=2015-09-28|access-date=2016-01-27}} However, on January 25, it was announced that a preliminary injunction allowing the promotion to hold the event was denied by a federal judge. According to an order issued by New York district court judge Kimba M. Wood, the federal court must first rule on Zuffa's appeal on New York's MMA ban and won't interfere with the enforcement of state law.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/01/ny-federal-judge-denies-ufc-move-to-allow-madison-square-garden-event|title=NY federal judge denies UFC move to allow Madison Square Garden event|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2016-01-25|access-date=2016-01-27}} On February 6, the UFC confirmed the event would take place in [[Las Vegas, Nevada]].\n\nThe event was expected to be headlined by a [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] rematch between then champion [[Daniel Cormier]] and former champion [[Jon Jones]]. The pairing met previously at [[UFC 182]] in January 2015 with Jones defending his title via unanimous decision.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/01/ufc-182-results-jon-jones-tested-but-defends-title-against-daniel-cormier|title=UFC 182 results: Jon Jones tested, but defends title against Daniel Cormier|author=Steven Marrocco|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2015-01-04|access-date=2016-02-07}} Subsequent to that victory, Jones was stripped of the title and suspended indefinitely from the UFC in connection with a [[Jon Jones#Hit-and-run conviction|hit-and-run incident]] that he was involved in.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/4/28/8512255/jon-jones-stripped-of-title-daniel-cormier-vs-anthony-johnson-for|title=Jon Jones stripped of title, Daniel Cormier vs. Anthony Johnson for belt headlines UFC 187|author=Marc Raimondi|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2015-04-29|access-date=2016-02-07}} Cormier replaced him and went on to defeat [[Anthony Johnson (fighter)|Anthony Johnson]] at [[UFC 187]] to win the vacant title.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/05/ufc-187-results-daniel-cormier-wears-down-anthony-johnson-for-third-round-tap|title=UFC 187 results: Daniel Cormier wears down Anthony Johnson for third-round tap|author=Steven Marrocco|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2015-05-24|access-date=2016-02-07}}\n\nOn March 29, Jones was arrested in [[Albuquerque, New Mexico]] on a charge of violating his probation related to his hit-and-run incident in 2015. The arrest took place five days after Jones was cited on multiple traffic charges, including [[drag racing]], exhibition driving, [[weaving]], failing to properly display his license plate and modifying his [[exhaust pipe]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/03/ufc-197-headliner-jon-jones-arrested-on-probation-violation-after-drag-racing-citation|title=UFC 197 headliner Jon Jones arrested on probation violation after drag racing citation|author=Steven Marrocco|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2016-03-29|access-date=2016-03-31}} He agreed to a plea deal and was released on March 31. In a statement, the UFC announced it was \"disappointed\" with Jones, but the bout would continue as planned.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/03/ufc-disappointed-with-jon-jones-but-ufc-197-headliner-is-still-a-go|title=UFC 'disappointed' with Jon Jones, but UFC 197 headliner is still a go|author=Staff|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2016-03-31|access-date=2016-03-31}} However, on April 1, it was announced that Cormier injured his foot and was out of the planned fight.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/04/rematch-on-hold-injury-forces-daniel-cormier-off-ufc-197-headliner-vs-jon-jones|title=Rematch on hold: Injury forces Daniel Cormier off UFC 197 headliner vs. Jon Jones|author=Staff|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2016-04-01|access-date=2016-04-02}} He was replaced by [[Ovince Saint Preux]] in what became an interim title fight.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/04/ovince-saint-preux-replaces-daniel-cormier-meets-jon-jones-in-new-ufc-197-headliner|title=Ovince Saint Preux replaces Daniel Cormier, meets Jon Jones in new UFC 197 headliner|author=Staff|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2016-04-02|access-date=2016-04-02}} Anthony Johnson was the original planned replacement, but due to a recent oral procedure, he wasn't able to accept the fight.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/04/why-anthony-johnson-isnt-fighting-jon-jones-on-short-notice-at-ufc-197|title=Why Anthony Johnson isn't fighting Jon Jones on short notice at UFC 197|author=Mike Bohn|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2016-04-03|access-date=2016-04-03}}\n\nA [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Demetrious Johnson (fighter)|Demetrious Johnson]] and [[Wrestling at the 2008 Summer Olympics – Men's freestyle 55 kg|2008 Olympic gold medalist in freestyle wrestling]] [[Henry Cejudo]] co-headlined the event.\n\nIn January 2016, [[B.J. Penn]] announced his intentions to return to active competition in the featherweight division after an 18-month \"retirement\" and was expected to compete at this event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/01/ex-champ-b-j-penn-ends-retirement-but-ufc-boss-still-unsure-on-details|title=Ex-champ B.J. Penn ends retirement, but UFC boss still unsure on details|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-01-19|access-date=2016-01-19}}{{cite web|url=http://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/ufc-bj-penn-says-his-return-has-been-pushed-back-to-ufc-197-in-april-020816|title=BJ Penn says his return has been pushed back to UFC 197 in April|work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2016-02-08|access-date=2016-02-08}} However, Penn's return was delayed after an investigation into recent criminal allegations made against him was launched.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/2/18/11048090/ufc-postpones-b-j-penns-return-amid-allegations|title=UFC postpones BJ Penn's return amid allegations|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=MMA Fighting Newswire|date=2016-02-18|access-date=2016-02-18}} After he was exonerated, the UFC announced that he would face [[Dennis Siver]] at [[UFC 199]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/04/b-j-penn-vs-dennis-siver-slated-for-ufc-199-in-los-angeles|title=B.J. Penn vs. Dennis Siver slated for UFC 199 in Los Angeles|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-04-12|access-date=2016-04-12}}\n\nFormer [[List of WSOF champions#Women.27s Strawweight Championship|WSOF Women's Strawweight Champion]] [[Jessica Aguilar]] was expected to face [[Juliana Lima]] at the event, but pulled out on March 18 due to a knee injury.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/3/18/11265262/jessica-aguilar-injured-out-of-ufc-197-fight-with-juliana-lima|title=Jessica Aguilar injured, out of UFC 197 fight with Juliana Lima|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2016-03-18|access-date=2016-03-18}} She was replaced by former [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship|UFC Women's Strawweight Champion]] [[Carla Esparza]].{{cite web|url=http://www.dailynews.com/sports/20160329/ufc-197-carla-esparza-returns-to-octagon-vs-julianna-lima|title=UFC 197: Carla Esparza returns to Octagon vs. Julianna Lima|publisher=dailynews.com|author=Brian Martin|date=2016-03-29|access-date=2016-03-29}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Heavyweight\n|[[Jon web|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/UFC-197-Bonuses-Johnson-Rodriguez-Roberts-Steele-Pocket-3650K-Awards-103835|title=UFC 197 bonuses: Johnson, Rodriguez, Roberts, Steele pocket $50k '''Demetrious Johnson''' and '''Yair Rodríguez'''\n\n==Reported payout==\nThe following is the reported payout to the fighters as reported to the [[Nevada Athletic Commission|Nevada State Athletic Commission]]. vs. dos Santos]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Hunt vs. Mir''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 85''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on March 20, 2016, at the [[Brisbane Entertainment Centre]] in [[Brisbane]], Australia.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/11/ufc-returning-to-australia-for-ufc-fight-night-84-in-brisbane-on-march-19|title=UFC returning to Australia for UFC Fight Night 84 in Brisbane on March 19|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Matt Erickson and John Morgan|date=2015-11-15|access-date=2015-11-15}}{{cite web|url=http://www.foxsports.com.au/ufc/mark-hunt-v-frank-mir-ufc-heavyweight-blockbuster-confirmed-for-brisbane-in-march/story-fnfmik1h-1227701736571|title=Mark Hunt v Frank Mir: UFC heavyweight blockbuster confirmed for Brisbane in March|publisher=foxsports.com.au|author=Nathan Ryan|date=8 January 2016|access-date=8 January 2016}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the second that the promotion hosted in [[Brisbane]], the first being [[UFC Fight Night: Hunt vs. Bigfoot]] in December 2013.\n\nIt was headlined by a heavyweight bout between the [[K-1 World Grand Prix 2001 Final|2001 K-1 World Grand Prix]] and former interim title contender [[Mark Hunt]] and former two-time [[UFC Heavyweight Champion]] [[Frank Mir]].\n\nA women's strawweight bout between [[Bec Rawlings]] and [[Seo Hee Ham]] was originally booked for [[UFC Fight Night: Miocic vs. Hunt]]. However, the bout was cancelled due to Ham being injured.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/04/lisa-ellis-replaces-seo-hee-ham-meets-bec-rawlings-at-ufc-fight-night-65|title=Lisa Ellis replaces Seo Hee Ham, meets Bec Rawlings at UFC Fight Night 65|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-04-10|access-date=2016-01-21}} The fight was later rescheduled for this event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/01/seo-hee-ham-vs-bec-rawlings-added-to-ufc-fight-night-84-in-australia|title=Seo Hee Ham vs. Bec Rawlings added to UFC Fight Night 84 in Australia|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-01-21|access-date=2016-01-21}}\n\n[[Justin Scoggins]] was expected to face [[Ben Nguyen]] at the event. However, Scoggins pulled out of the fight in the week leading up to the event citing injury. As a result, Nguyen was pulled from the card entirely.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/03/nguyen-vs-scoggins-scrapped-laprise-now-meets-pearson-in-ufc-fight-night-85-shuffle|title=Nguyen vs. Scoggins scrapped, Laprise now meets Pearson in UFC Fight Night 85 shuffle|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-03-12|access-date=2016-03-12}}\n\n[[Abel Trujillo]] was expected to face [[Ross Pearson]] at the event. However, Trujillo was removed from the card on March 12 due to alleged [[visa (document)|visa]] issues which restricted his entry to Australia. As a result, Pearson faced [[Chad Laprise]]. Subsequently, Laprise's initial opponent [[Alan Patrick (fighter)|Alan Patrick]] remained on the card against promotional newcomer [[Damien Brown (fighter)|Damien Brown]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/03/australias-damien-brown-gets-fill-in-duties-at-saturdays-ufc-fight-night-85|title=Australia's Damien Brown gets fill-in duties at Saturday's UFC Fight Night 85|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-03-14|access-date=2016-03-14}}\n\nOn April 8, it was announced by [[USADA]] that Frank Mir was informed of a potential anti-doping policy violation stemming from an in-competition sample collected the day of the event.{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-statement-on-frank-mir-april-8-2016?redirect=no|title=UFC statement on Frank Mir|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2016-04-08|access-date=2016-04-08}} Mir released a statement denying the use of any performance-enhancing drugs (PED) and he didn't know how he could have tested positive for any substance.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/4/8/11396356/frank-mir-releases-statement-on-potential-usada-anti-doping-policy|title=Frank Mir releases statement on potential USADA anti-doping policy violation|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-04-08|access-date=2016-04-08}} He revealed that he tested positive for a small amount of [[4-Chlorodehydromethyltestosterone|Oral Turinabol]] and denied knowingly taking the substance. Mir also admitted he will likely retire if he is suspended two years, the standard suspension for first time offenders.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/4/9/11399222/frank-mir-my-career-is-likely-over-if-usada-suspends-me-for-two-years|title=Frank Mir: My career is likely over if USADA suspends me for two years|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-04-09|access-date=2016-04-10}}\n\nA few days later, [[Viscardi Andrade]] was also notified of a potential anti-doping violation stemming from an out-of-competition sample collection on March 7. The sample was collected in [[Brazil]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/04/viscardi-andrade-notified-of-potential-usada-anti-doping-violation-from-ufc-fight-night-85|title=Viscardi Andrade notified of potential USADA anti-doping violation from UFC Fight Night 85|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-04-12|access-date=2016-04-12}} Ultimately, the bout was overturned to a no-contest, and Viscardi was given a two-year suspension.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/2/27/17058366/viscardi-andrade-regrets-asking-ufc-release-usada-suspension|title=Viscardi Andrade regrets asking for UFC release during USADA suspension|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2018-02-27|access-date=2018-08-14}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent bout between [[Ben Rothwell]] and former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight Champion]] [[Junior dos Santos]] served as the event headliner.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/02/ben-rothwell-vs-junior-dos-santos-headlines-ufc-fight-night-86-in-croatia|title=Ben Rothwell vs. Junior dos Santos headlines UFC Fight Night 86 in However, Mihajlović was removed from the card on March 14 due to undisclosed reasons and was replaced by fellow promotional newcomer [[Curtis Blaydes]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/03/curtis-blaydes-in-for-bojan-mihajlovic-against-francis-ngannou-at-ufc-fight-night-86|title=Curtis Blaydes in for Bojan Mihajlovic against Francis Ngannou at UFC held on May 8, 2016, at the [[Rotterdam Ahoy]] in [[Rotterdam, Netherlands]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Overeem-Arlovski-Headline-UFC-Debut-Event-in-The-Netherlands|title=Overeem and Arlovski to headline UFC debut in The Netherlands|publisher=ufc.com|author=UFC Press Release|date=2016-02-26|access-date=2016-02-26}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the first that the organization hosted in the [[Netherlands]].\n\nThe event was headlined by a heavyweight bout between the [[List of K-1 champions|2010 K-1 World Grand Prix Champion]] and former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Heavyweight Championship|Strikeforce Heavyweight Champion]] [[Alistair Overeem]] and former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight Champion]] [[Andrei Arlovski]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2016/2/20/11078924/alistair-overeem-vs-andrei-arlovski-heavyweight-main-event-set-ufc-fgiht-night-87-rotterdam-mma|title=Alistair Overeem vs Andrei Arlovski heavyweight main event set for UFC Fight Night 87 in Rotterdam|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Brad Popkin|date=2016-02-20|access-date=2016-02-20}}\n\n[[Rashid Magomedov]] was expected to face [[Chris Wade (fighter)|Chris Wade]] at the event. However, Magomedov pulled out of the fight in early March citing injury and was replaced by [[Rustam Khabilov]].{{cite web|url=http://mmanytt.se/galor/ufc/ufn-87-rustam-khabilov-tar-sig-chris-wade-rotterdam/|title=UFN 87: Rustam Khabilov tackles Chris Wade in Rotterdam (Swedish)|publisher=mmanytt.se|author=Sebastian Vendel-Martinez|date=2016-03-08|access-date=2016-03-11}}\n\n[[Peter Sobotta]] was expected to face Dominic Waters at the event. However, Sobotta pulled out of the fight in late March citing injury and was replaced by [[Leon Edwards]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/03/leon-edwards-steps-in-for-injured-peter-sobotta-at-ufc-fight-night-87-in-rotterdam|title=Leon Edwards steps in for injured Peter Sobotta at UFC Fight Night 87 in Rotterdam|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-03-30|access-date=2016-03-30}}\n\n[[Paddy Holohan]] was expected to face [[Willie Gates]] at the event. However, on April 25, Holohan abruptly announced his retirement, citing a [[Factor XIII deficiency|rare blood disorder]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/paddy-holohan-announces-retirement-april-25-2016|title=Paddy Holohan announces retirement|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2016-04-25|access-date=2016-04-16}} In turn, Gates faced [[Ulka Sasaki]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/04/with-paddy-holohans-retirement-willie-gates-gets-new-ufc-fight-night-87-opponent|title=With Paddy Holohan's retirement, Willie Gates gets new UFC Fight Night 87 opponent|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-04-26|access-date=2016-04-26}}\n\n[[Nick Hein]] was expected to face [[Jon Tuck]] on the card, but pulled out just 6 days before the event due to injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/05/nick-hein-injured-ufc-seeks-replacement-opponent-for-jon-tuck-at-sundays-ufc-fight-night-87-event|title=Nick Hein injured, UFC seeks replacement opponent for Jon Tuck at Sunday's UFC Fight Night 87 event|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2016-05-02|access-date=2016-05-04}} He was replaced two days later by promotional newcomer [[Josh Emmett]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/05/team-alpha-males-josh-emmett-in-for-nick-hein-vs-jon-tuck-at-ufc-fight-night-87|title=Team Alpha Male's Josh Emmett in for Nick Hein vs. Jon Tuck description|UFC mixed martial arts event in 2016}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=UFC on Fox: Teixeira vs. Evans\n|image=Nurmagomedov_vs._Ferguson.jpg|venue= [[Amalie Arena]]\n|city= [[Tampa, Florida]]\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date= April 16, 2016\n\n|attendance=11,273{{cite Jones vs. Saint Preux]]\n}}\n'''''UFC on Fox: Teixeira vs. Evans''''' (also known as '''''UFC on Fox 19''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on April 16, 2016, at [[Amalie Arena]] in [[Tampa, Florida]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/02/ufc-on-fox-19-official-for-april-16-in-tampa-with-nurmagomedov-ferguson-main-event|title=UFC on FOX 19 official for April 16 in Tampa with Nurmagomedov-Ferguson main event|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author= Mike Bohn and Matt Erickson|date=2016-02-07|access-date=2016-02-07}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the second that the organization has hosted in the [[Tampa Bay Area]], and the first since [[UFC Fight Night: Lauzon vs. Stephens]] in February 2009.\n\nIt was expected to be headlined by a potential [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] title eliminator bout between undefeated [[Khabib Nurmagomedov]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Lesnar vs. Team dos Santos]] winner [[Tony Ferguson]]. The fight was previously scheduled for [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team McGregor vs. Team Faber#The Ultimate Fighter 22 Finale|The Ultimate Fighter: Team McGregor vs. Team Faber Finale]], but Nurmagomedov pulled out due to a rib injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/10/khabib-nurmagomedov-injured-out-of-tuf-22-finale-bout-vs-tony-ferguson|title=Khabib Nurmagomedov injured, out of TUF 22 Finale bout vs. Tony Ferguson|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2015-10-30|access-date=2016-04-05}} The fight was eventually rescheduled for this event. On April 5, it was announced that Ferguson pulled out of the bout due to a [[lung]] issue. Therefore, the Teixeira-Evans bout was elevated to a five-round main event status.{{cite web|url=http://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/ufc-tony-ferguson-out-rashad-evans-vs-glover-teixeira-set-as-new-main-event-040516|title=Tony Ferguson out; Rashad Evans vs. Glover Teixeira set as new main event|publisher=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2016-04-05|access-date=2016-04-05}} Two days later, it was announced that Nurmagomedov would face promotional newcomer [[Darrell Horcher]] in a catchweight of 160 lb.{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2016/4/7/11388238/mma-news-khabib-nurmagomedov-to-face-newcomer-darrell-horcher-ufc-on-fox-19|title=Khabib Nurmagomedov to face newcomer Darrell Horcher at UFC on FOX 19|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Mookie Alexander|date=2016-04-07|access-date=2016-04-07|archive-date=2016-04-10|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160410065337/http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2016/4/7/11388238/mma-news-khabib-nurmagomedov-to-face-newcomer-darrell-horcher-ufc-on-fox-19|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Maurício Rua]] was expected to face [[Rashad Evans]] in a bout between two former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship|UFC Light Heavyweight Champions]]. The pairing was originally expected to meet in Rua's first title defense in March 2011 at [[UFC 128]], but Evans pulled out due to a knee injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/22361/rashad-evans-hurt-jon-jones-now-challenges-shogun-rua-for-title-at-ufc-128|title=Rashad Evans hurt, Jon Jones now challenges Shogun Rua for title at UFC 128|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2011-02-05|access-date=2016-03-09}}{{Dead link|date=January 2026 |bot=InternetArchiveBot }} Once again the bout did not materialize, as Rua pulled out of the fight on March 9 due to injury and was replaced by former title challenger [[Glover Teixeira]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/03/glover-teixeira-in-for-injured-shogun-rua-vs-rashad-evans-at-ufc-on-fox-19-in-tampa-1|title=Glover Teixeira in for injured 'Shogun' Rua vs. Rashad Evans at UFC on FOX 19 in Tampa|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-03-09|access-date=2016-03-09}}\n\n[[Caio Magalhães]] was expected to face [[Cezar Ferreira]] at the event. Magalhães pulled out of the fight in the week leading up to the event citing an ankle injury and was replaced by [[Oluwale Bamgbose]].{{cite web|url=http://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/ufc-caio-magalhaes-out-oluwale-bambose-faces-cezar-ferreira-in-tampa-040916|title=Caio Magalhães out; Oluwale Bamgbose to face Cezar Ferreira in Tampa|publisher=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2016-04-09|access-date=2016-04-09}}\n\nA rematch between former light heavyweight champion [[Lyoto Machida]] and former [[List of Pride champions#Pride welterweight champion|Pride Welterweight]] and [[List of Pride champions#Pride middleweight champion|Middleweight Champion]] as well as former [[List of Strikeforce champions|Strikeforce Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Dan Henderson]] was expected to take place at this event. Their first fight ended in a split decision win for Machida at [[UFC 157]] in February 2013.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2013/2/23/4023350/ufc-157-results-lyoto-machida-beats-dan-henderson|title=UFC 157 results: Lyoto Machida beats Dan Henderson|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Chiappetta|date=2013-02-23|access-date=2016-04-13}} However, on April 13, the UFC announced that Machida declared the usage of a banned substance during an out-of-competition sample collection last week. Machida stated that he was unaware that the substance was prohibited both in and out of competition and, in accordance with the UFC Anti-Doping Policy, his disclosure of usage will be taken into consideration by [[USADA]] during any potential results management and adjudication process. Therefore, he was removed from the bout and Henderson was re-booked for another event at a later date.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/04/lyoto-machida-admits-use-of-banned-substance-fight-with-dan-henderson-scrapped-at-ufc-on-fox-19|title=Machida admits use of banned substance, UFC on FOX 19 fight with Henderson scrapped|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-04-13|access-date=2016-04-13}} He later released a statement announcing that he used a supplement which contains [[7-Keto-DHEA]]. He also claimed ignorance in the situation and apologized for it, as well as accepting full responsibility for his actions.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/04/lyoto-machida-apologizes-for-ufc-on-fox-19-removal-says-he-was-unaware-of-banned-substance|title=Lyoto Machida apologizes for UFC removal, says he was unaware substance banned|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2016-04-13|access-date=2016-04-13}}\n\nAfter the weigh-ins, the UFC announced [[Islam Makhachev]] was informed by [[USADA]] of a potential anti-doping policy violation involving [[meldonium]]. Therefore, he received a provisional suspension and was removed from his scheduled bout against [[Drew Dober]]. Additional information will be provided at the appropriate time as the process moves forward.{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-Statement-on-Islam-Makhachev|title=UFC statement on Islam Makhachev|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2016-04-15|access-date=2016-04-15}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Fight Card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Heavyweight\n|[[Glover =$8,197,628{{cite web|url=http://boxing.nv.gov/results/Top_MMA_Gates|title=Top MMA Gates|date=September 16, 2016|publisher=Nevada State Athletic Commission}}\n|previousevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Silva vs. Bisping]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Hunt vs. Mir]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 196: McGregor vs. Diaz''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on March 5, 2016, at [[MGM Grand Garden Arena]] in [[Las Vegas]], [[Nevada]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/09/ufcs-2016-first-quarter-schedule-shapes-up-including-ufc-196-on-feb-6-in-las-vegas|title=UFC's 2016 first-quarter schedule shapes up, including UFC 196 on Feb. 6 in Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-09-16|access-date=2016-01-08}}{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/01/report-rafael-dos-anjos-conor-mcgregor-holly-holm-miesha-tate-set-for-ufc-197-in-las-vegas|title=Report: Rafael dos Anjos-Conor McGregor, Holly Holm-Miesha Tate for UFC 197, Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-01-07|access-date=2016-01-07}}{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-197-announced-conor-mcgregor-versus-rafael-dos-anjos-and-holly-holm-vs-miesha-tate|title=It's on! dos Anjos/McGregor, Holm/Tate set for UFC 197 |publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2016-01-12|access-date=2016-01-12}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was officially announced in November 2015 as \"UFC 197\", taking place on March 5, 2016, at [[HSBC Arena (Rio de Janeiro)|HSBC Arena]] in [[Rio de Janeiro]], [[Brazil]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/11/ufc-197-set-for-hsbc-arena-in-rio-de-janeiro-on-march-5|title=UFC 197 set for HSBC Arena in Rio de Janeiro on March 5|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2015-11-08|access-date=2016-01-08}} Weeks later on December 29, [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]] announced that the event was canceled and would be moved to a different location.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/12/29/10684682/ufc-cancels-event-in-rio-de-janeiro-due-to-economic-crisis|title=Anderson Silva: UFC cancels event in Rio de Janeiro|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2015-12-29|access-date=2016-01-08}} On January 12, it was confirmed the event would take place at the [[MGM Grand Garden Arena]] in [[Las Vegas Valley|Las Vegas]], [[Nevada]], United States.\n\nOn January 13, UFC revealed this event was originally intended to take place at [[Arena da Baixada]] (a much larger venue) in [[Curitiba]], [[Paraná (state)|Paraná]], [[Brazil]], and feature a [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]] rematch between champion [[Fabrício Werdum]] and former two-time champion [[Cain Velasquez]], with former [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] [[Anderson Silva]] in the co-main event slot, possibly against [[Michael Bisping]]. Due to a series of injuries, the company moved the heavyweight bout to a separate event scheduled for February 6, which at that time was [[UFC Fight Night: Hendricks vs. Thompson|titled \"UFC 196\"]]. This change made the March 5 event a smaller draw, which led to the transition to the smaller venue in [[Rio de Janeiro]]. To maintain local interest, the company then considered booking the main event as a rematch between two [[Brazilians|Brazilian]] fighters, Silva and former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship|UFC Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Vitor Belfort]]. Belfort declined the bout, as he wished to wait for the result of the [[Middleweight (MMA)|middleweight]] title fight at [[UFC 194]]. This final fall-through is what prompted the cancellation of the event in Brazil and relocation to [[Las Vegas]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/1/13/10761276/ufc-official-confirms-vitor-belfort-turned-down-fight-with-anderson|title=UFC official confirms Vitor Belfort turned down fight with Anderson Silva at UFC 197|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2016-01-13|access-date=2016-01-13}} On January 27, it was revealed the Werdum-Velasquez bout (which had been moved to \"UFC 196\" scheduled for February 6, 2016) was cancelled entirely. This forced that event to alter into a \"UFC Fight Night\" card, and the originally scheduled \"UFC 197\" event was renamed UFC 196.{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Hendricks-Thompson-headlines-UFC-Fight-Night|title=Hendricks-Thompson headlines UFC Fight Night|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2016-01-27|access-date=2016-01-27}}\n\nThe event was expected to be headlined by a [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] bout between champion [[Rafael dos Anjos]] and [[UFC Featherweight Championship|UFC Featherweight Champion]] [[Conor McGregor]]. This would have been the second time in [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]] history that champions in different divisions would fight for the same title. The first time was at [[UFC 94]] on January 31, 2009, when then-[[UFC Welterweight Championship|UFC Welterweight Champion]] [[Georges St-Pierre]] defended his title against then-lightweight champion [[B.J. Penn]]. On February 23, the planned bout suffered a setback as it was announced that dos Anjos pulled out due to a broken foot.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/2/23/11098398/rafael-dos-anjos-injured-out-of-ufc-196-main-event-fight-against|title=Rafael dos Anjos injured, out of UFC 196 main event fight against Conor McGregor|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2016-02-23|access-date=2016-02-23}} Former [[UFC Featherweight Championship|UFC Featherweight Champion]] [[José Aldo]] declined the initial opportunity to replace him due to \"lack of time\" to prepare for the bout.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/2/23/11099670/coach-jose-aldo-not-in-shape-turned-down-offer-to-replace-rafael-dos|title=Coach: Jose Aldo not in shape, turned down offer to replace Rafael dos Anjos at UFC 196|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2016-02-23|access-date=2016-02-23}} Former lightweight champion and top featherweight contender [[Frankie Edgar]] also declined the opportunity due to a groin injury.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/2/24/11105514/frankie-edgar-pissed-at-ufc-says-the-c-in-ufc-stands-for-conor|title=Frankie Edgar 'pissed' at UFC, says the 'C' in UFC stands for 'Conor'|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Chuck Mindenhall|date=2016-02-24|access-date=2016-02-24}} Eventually, [[The Ultimate Fighter 5]] winner and former lightweight title challenger [[Nate Diaz]] was announced as the replacement and the bout took place in the welterweight division.{{cite news|url=http://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/ufc-196-conor-mcgregor-nate-diaz-new-main-event-022316|title=Nate Diaz gets the call, faces Conor McGregor in new UFC 196 main event|work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2016-02-23|access-date=2016-02-23}}\n\nA [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]] bout between then champion [[Holly Holm]] and former [[Strikeforce Women's Bantamweight Championship|Strikeforce Women's Bantamweight Champion]] [[Miesha Tate]] co-headlined the event.\n\n[[Michael Johnson (fighter)|Michael Johnson]] was expected to face [[Tony Ferguson]] in a lightweight rematch. On January 27, it was announced that Johnson withdrew from the bout due to injury. Ferguson was then rescheduled to fight at [[UFC on Fox: Teixeira vs. Evans|UFC on Fox 19]] on April 16 against [[Khabib Nurmagomedov]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/1/26/10838404/khabib-nurmagomedov-vs-tony-ferguson-targeted-for-april-ufc-on-fox?_ga=1.211413789.94603769.1452949280|title=Khabib Nurmagomedov vs. Tony Ferguson targeted for April UFC on Fox event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Hunter A. Homistek|date=2016-01-26|access-date=2016-01-27}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Brandon Thatch]] and [[Siyar Bahadurzada]], originally slated for [[UFC Fight Night: Cowboy vs. Cowboy]], was rescheduled to this event due to Thatch's medication being banned by Pennsylvania's athletic commission.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/02/medication-clearance-issue-moves-siyar-bahadurzada-vs-brandon-thatch-to-ufc-196|title='Medication' clearance issue moves Siyar Bahadurzada vs. Brandon Thatch to UFC 196|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-02-12|access-date=2016-02-12}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Welterweight \n|[[Nate Almeida]] and [[Cody Garbrandt]] headlined the event.\n\nKeith Berish was very briefly linked to a bout with [[Jake Collier]] at the event. However, Berish pulled out due to injury on March 31 and was replaced by UFC newcomer Alberto Uda.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/3/31/11338996/undefeated-alberto-uda-signs-with-the-ufc-replaces-keith-berish-vs|title=Undefeated Alberto Uda signs with the UFC, replaces Keith Berish vs. Jake Collier at UFC Fight Night 88|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2016-03-31|access-date=2016-03-31}}\n\n[[Joe Proctor]] was expected to face [[Erik Koch]] at the event. In accordance with the UFC Anti-Doping Policy, Ferreira has received a provisional suspension. Additional violation information will be provided at the appropriate time as the process moves forward.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/05/diego-ferreira-removed-from-ufc-fight-night-88-due-to-potential-usada-anti-doping-violation|title=Diego Ferreira removed from UFC Fight Night 88 due to potential
