martial arts]] event held on June 4, 2016, at [[The Forum (Inglewood, California)|The Forum]] in [[Inglewood, California]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/3/4/11161566/luke-rockhold-vs-chris-weidman-dominick-cruz-vs-urijah-faber-headline|title=Luke Rockhold vs. Chris Weidman, Dominick Cruz vs. Urijah Faber headline UFC 199|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-03-04|access-date=2016-03-04}}\n\n==Background==\nWhile this was the first time the organization has held an event at the venue, it was the fifth time that UFC has hosted an event in [[Los Angeles County, California|Los Angeles County]]. Previous UFC events in the area took place at the [[Staples Center]].\n\nThe event was expected to be headlined by a [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] rematch between current champion [[Luke Rockhold]] and former champion [[Chris Weidman]]. They previously met at [[UFC 194]], when Rockhold won Weidman's title via fourth-round TKO.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/12/13/10021662/ufc-194-results-luke-rockhold-stuns-chris-weidman-to-win-middleweight|title=UFC 194 results: Luke Rockhold stuns Chris Weidman to win middleweight title|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2015-12-13|access-date=2016-03-17}} However, Weidman pulled out on May 17 due to a [[spinal disc herniation|large extrusion herniation]] on his neck.{{cite news|url=http://www.flocombat.com/article/41884-chris-weidman-out-of-ufc-199-bout-with-luke-rockhold|title=Chris Weidman out of UFC 199 bout with Luke Rockhold|publisher=flocombat.com|author=Jeremy Botter|date=2016-05-17|access-date=2016-05-17}}{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/05/chris-weidman-explains-injury-that-forced-him-out-of-ufc-199-title-fight|title=Chris Weidman explains injury that forced him out of UFC 199 title fight|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-05-18|access-date=2016-05-18}} The first replacement choice was former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Middleweight Championship|Strikeforce Middleweight Champion]] [[Ronaldo Souza]], who lost his title to Rockhold in 2011, but he injured his [[Meniscus (anatomy)|meniscus]] in a win at [[UFC 198]] against former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship|UFC Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Vitor Belfort]]. Therefore, he was replaced by [[The Ultimate Fighter 3]] winner [[Michael Bisping]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/5/18/11702070/michael-bisping-in-to-face-luke-rockhold-at-ufc-199-with-chris|title=Michael Bisping in to face Luke Rockhold at UFC 199 with Chris Weidman out|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-05-18|access-date=2016-05-18}} The fight was also a rematch as the two met previously in November 2014 at [[UFC Fight Night: Rockhold vs. Bisping]], with Rockhold winning via submission in the second round.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/11/ufc-fight-night-55-results-luke-rockhold-taps-michael-bisping-with-one-armed-guillotine|title=UFC Fight Night 55 results: Luke Rockhold taps Michael Bisping with one-armed guillotine|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Ben Fowlkes|date=2014-11-07|access-date=2016-05-18}}\n\nThe co-main event featured a [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] trilogy bout between current two-time champion [[Dominick Cruz]] and former [[List of WEC champions#Featherweight Championship|WEC Featherweight Champion]] [[Urijah Faber]]. The pairing first met at featherweight on [[2007 in WEC#WEC 26: Condit vs. Alessio|WEC 26]] in 2007, where Faber defeated Cruz via guillotine choke to defend his title.{{cite news|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2013/8/22/4639590/throwback-thursday-urijah-faber-vs-dominick-cruz-wec-26-2007-video|title=Throwback Thursday: Urijah Faber vs Dominick Cruz, WEC 26 (March 24, 2007)|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=T.P. Grant|date=2013-08-22|access-date=2016-03-17|archive-date=2016-03-25|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160325175800/http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2013/8/22/4639590/throwback-thursday-urijah-faber-vs-dominick-cruz-wec-26-2007-video|url-status=dead}} The rematch took place on [[UFC 132]] in 2011, when Cruz defended his title for the first time via unanimous decision.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2011/07/ufc-132-play-by-play-and-live-results|title=UFC 132 play by play and live results|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2011-07-02|access-date=2016-03-17}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Evan Dunham]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil 2]] winner [[Leonardo Santos (fighter)|Leonardo Santos]], originally scheduled for [[UFC 198]], was moved to this event after a minor injury to Dunham delayed the matchup a few weeks.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/3/15/11242150/evan-dunham-suffers-small-injury-fight-with-leonardo-santos-moved-to|title=Evan Dunham suffers 'small injury', fight with Leonardo Santos moved to UFC 199|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2016-03-15|access-date=2016-03-16}} Subsequently, Santos pulled out on April 29 due to an undisclosed injury and was replaced by [[James Vick]].{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Friday-Fight-Update-043016|title=Friday fight update: Vick, Sasaki get fights|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2016-04-29|access-date=2016-04-29}} In turn, Dunham pulled out of that pairing on May 5 for undisclosed reasons and was replaced by [[Beneil Dariush]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/5/6/11611726/james-vick-vs-beneil-dariush-targeted-for-ufc-199|title=James Vick vs. Beneil Dariush targeted for UFC 199|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2016-05-06|access-date=2016-05-06}}\n\nIn January 2016, former [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight]] and [[UFC Welterweight Championship|Welterweight Champion]] [[B.J. Penn]] announced his intention to return to active competition in the featherweight division after an 18-month \"retirement\" and was expected to compete at [[UFC 197]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/01/ex-champ-b-j-penn-ends-retirement-but-ufc-boss-still-unsure-on-details|title=Ex-champ B.J. Penn ends retirement, but UFC boss still unsure on details|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-01-19|access-date=2016-04-12}}{{cite web|url=http://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/ufc-bj-penn-says-his-return-has-been-pushed-back-to-ufc-197-in-april-020816|title=BJ Penn says his return has been pushed back to UFC 197 in April|work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2016-02-08|access-date=2016-04-12}} However, Penn's return was delayed after an investigation into criminal allegations made against him was launched.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/2/18/11048090/ufc-postpones-b-j-penns-return-amid-allegations|title=UFC postpones BJ Penn's return amid allegations|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=MMA Fighting Newswire|date=2016-02-18|access-date=2016-04-12}} After the UFC concluded that no criminal or civil charges would be filed, the organization announced that he would face [[Dennis Siver]] at this event.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/04/b-j-penn-vs-dennis-siver-slated-for-ufc-199-in-los-angeles|title=B.J. Penn vs. Dennis Siver slated for UFC 199 in Los Angeles|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-04-12|access-date=2016-04-12}}{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-Statement-on-BJ-Penn-041316|title=UFC statement on BJ Penn|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2016-04-13|access-date=2016-04-13}} In turn, Siver pulled out on May 10 due to an undisclosed injury.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/5/11/11654852/ufc-199-fight-card-dennis-siver-injured-vs-b-j-penn|title=Dennis Siver injured, out of UFC 199 fight opposite B.J. Penn|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|date=2016-05-11|access-date=2016-05-11}} He was replaced by [[Cole Miller]].{{cite news|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Cole-Miller-Steps-in-to-Face-BJ-Penn-at-UFC-199-in-Los-Angeles-104807|title=Cole Miller steps in to face B.J. Penn at UFC 199 in Los Angeles|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2016-05-11|access-date=2016-05-11}} Subsequently, Penn himself was removed from the card on May 23 after he was flagged for a potential anti-doping violation: the use of an [[Intravenous therapy|IV]] in excess of 50 ml in a six-hour period during a March 25 out-of-competition sample collection.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2016/5/23/11756738/bj-penn-flagged-anti-doping-violation-iv-ban-ufc-199-cole-miller-mma|title=BJ Penn flagged for possible anti-doping violation, pulled from UFC 199 fight with Cole Miller|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Thomas Myers|date=2016-05-23|access-date=2016-05-23}} He was replaced by [[Alex Caceres]].{{cite news|url=http://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/ufc-alex-caceres-replaces-bj-penn-faces-cole-miller-at-ufc-199-052516|title=Alex Caceres replaces BJ Penn, faces Cole Miller at UFC 199|work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2016-05-25|access-date=2016-05-25}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[John Makdessi]] and [[Mehdi Baghdad]] was originally scheduled for this event, but on April 28 it was announced that the bout was moved to [[UFC Fight Night: dos Anjos vs. Alvarez]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/04/mehdi-baghdad-vs-john-makdessi-moves-from-ufc-199-in-june-to-ufc-fight-night-90-in-july|title=Mehdi Baghdad vs. John Makdessi moves from UFC 199 in June to UFC Fight Night 90 in July|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2016-04-28|access-date=2016-04-28}}\n\nThe event marked the first time fighters were allowed to weigh in for their bouts in the morning (starting 10 a.m.), rather than the traditional time of 4 p.m. used for public proceedings. They weighed in with a [[California State Athletic Commission]] (CSAC) official looking on so they would have less time to be dehydrated after their weight cuts and more time to rehydrate after making weight. They were back on hand to stand on the scale – ceremoniously, for the fans and media – and face off with their opponents. This is part of new measures for fighters' safety, with the CSAC creating new rules that will eventually be adapted to all the other athletic commissions.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/06/ufc-199-live-and-official-weigh-in-results-2|title=UFC 199 weigh-in results: Rockhold-Bisping, Cruz-Faber official for title fights|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-06-03|access-date=2016-06-04}}\n\nDuring the main card broadcast, the UFC announced that [[UFC 8]] and [[Ultimate Ultimate 96]] tournaments winner [[Don Frye]] will be inducted to the UFC's Hall of Fame at the \"International Fight Week\" in July, one day after [[UFC 200]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/06/mma-legend-don-frye-set-for-induction-into-ufc-hall-of-fame|title=MMA legend Don Frye set for induction into UFC Hall of Fame|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2016-06-04|access-date=2016-06-04}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Middleweight\n|[[Michael Bisping]]\n|def.\n|[[Luke Rockhold]] (c)\n|KO Arlovski]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Almeida vs. Garbrandt]]\n}}\n'''''UFC 198: Werdum vs. Miocic''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on May 14, 2016, at [[Arena da Baixada]] in [[Curitiba]], [[Brazil]].{{cite news|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Fabricio-Werdum-vs-Stipe-Miocic-to-Headline-UFC-198-in-Brazil-100661|title=Fabricio Werdum vs. Stipe Miocic to headline UFC 198 in Brazil|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Marcelo Alonso|date=2016-02-22|access-date=2016-02-22}}{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/03/ufc-198-werdum-vs-miocic-official-for-may-14-in-40000-seat-brazilian-arena|title='UFC 198: Werdum vs. Miocic' official for May 14 in 40,000-seat Brazilian arena|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-03-04|access-date=2016-03-04}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was initially announced as a ''Fight Night'' event with a middleweight bout between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship|UFC Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Vitor Belfort]] and former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Middleweight Championship|Strikeforce Middleweight Champion]] [[Ronaldo Souza]] scheduled to serve as headliner.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/1/19/10794196/vitor-belfort-vs-jacare-souza-announced-for-ufc-event-in-brazil|title=Vitor Belfort vs. Jacare Souza announced for UFC event in Brazil|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|date=2016-01-19|access-date=2016-01-19}} However, it was reported by Brazilian media that this event would become UFC 198 following a series of events in January 2016 that led to [[UFC Fight Night: Hendricks vs. Thompson|the originally planned UFC 196]] becoming a UFC Fight Night following a series of cancellations.{{cite web|url=http://sportv.globo.com/site/combate/noticia/2016/02/ultimate-altera-edicao-no-brasil-para-ufc-198-e-quer-werdum-x-miocic.html|title=Ultimate altera edição no Brasil para UFC 198 e quer Werdum x Miocic (Brazilian Portuguese)|publisher=sportv.globo.com|author=Evelyn Rodrigues, Marcelo Barone and Raphael Marinho|access-date=2016-02-04|date=2016-02-04}}\n\nOn March 4, it was confirmed that the event was indeed changed to PPV and it would be headlined by a [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]] bout between champion [[Fabrício Werdum]] and top contender [[Stipe Miocic]]. They were briefly scheduled to meet at the [[UFC Fight Night: Hendricks vs. Thompson|original UFC 196]], as Miocic replaced former two-time champion [[Cain Velasquez]]. In turn, Werdum announced on the following day that he was pulling out of the event due to injury as well as he wasn't comfortable with the opponent change while dealing with injuries of his own.{{cite news|url=http://fansided.com/2016/01/24/cain-velasquez-out-fabricio-werdum-stipe-miocic-ufc-196/|title=Cain Velasquez out, Stipe Miocic to fill in at UFC 196|publisher=fansided.com|author=Mike Dyce|date=2016-01-24|access-date=2016-02-24}}{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/1/25/10828922/fabricio-werdum-pulls-out-of-ufc-196-with-injury|title=Fabricio Werdum pulls out of UFC 196 with injury|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2016-01-25|access-date=2016-02-24}}\n\nThe event was the first that the promotion hosted in [[Curitiba]]. It was the fourth [[stadium|stadium venue]] to host a UFC event after [[UFC 129]] at the [[Rogers Centre]] in [[Canada]], [[UFC on Fox: Gustafsson vs. Johnson]] at the [[Tele2 Arena]] in [[Sweden]] and [[UFC 193]] at the [[Docklands Stadium|Etihad Stadium]] in [[Australia]].\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Evan Dunham]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil 2]] winner [[Leonardo Santos (fighter)|Leonardo Santos]], originally scheduled for this event, was moved to [[UFC 199]] after a minor injury to Dunham delayed the matchup a few weeks.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/3/15/11242150/evan-dunham-suffers-small-injury-fight-with-leonardo-santos-moved-to|title=Evan Dunham suffers 'small injury', fight with Leonardo Santos moved to UFC 199|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2016-03-15|access-date=2016-03-15}}\n\nThe event featured the long anticipated debut of Curitiba native [[Cris Cyborg]], former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Women's Featherweight Championship|Strikeforce Women's Featherweight Champion]] and current [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Featherweight Championship|Invicta FC Featherweight Champion]], in a catchweight bout of 140 lb against [[Leslie Smith (fighter)|Leslie Smith]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/03/cyborg-justino-meets-leslie-smith-at-ufc-198-in-brazil|title='Cyborg' Justino meets Leslie Smith at UFC 198 in Brazil|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-03-28|access-date=2016-03-28}}\n\n[[Kamaru Usman]] was expected to face [[Sérgio Moraes]] at the event. However, he pulled out two weeks before the event due to injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Luan Chagas]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/05/kamaru-usman-out-of-ufc-198-bout-with-sergio-moraes|title=Kamaru Usman out of UFC 198 bout with Sergio Moraes|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-05-01|access-date=2016-05-01}}\n\nFormer [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] [[Anderson Silva]] was expected to face [[Uriah Hall]] at the event. However, Silva pulled out just four days before the event with a clinical condition that indicated acute [[cholecystitis]] and required surgical intervention.{{cite news|url=https://sports.yahoo.com/blogs/mma-cagewriter/anderson-silva-out-of-ufc-189--may-need-gall-bladder-surgery-005830365.html|title=Anderson Silva out of UFC 198, may require gall bladder surgery|publisher=sports.yahoo.com|author=Kevin Iole|date=2016-05-10|access-date=2016-05-10}} The UFC wanted to find a replacement for him, but Hall opted against that.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/5/11/11656276/uriah-hall-of-ufc-198-fight-card|title=Uriah Hall off UFC 198 fight card|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Luke Thomas|date=2016-05-11|access-date=2016-05-11}}\n\nDuring the main card broadcast, the UFC announced that former interim [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight Champion]] and [[Pride Heavyweight Championship|Pride Heavyweight Champion]] [[Antônio Rodrigo Nogueira]] would be inducted to the UFC's Hall of Fame at the \"International Fight Week\" in July, one day after [[UFC 200]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/05/ufc-announces-antonio-rodrigo-nogueira-as-newest-member-of-ufc-hall-of-fame|title=UFC announces Antônio Rodrigo Nogueira as newest member of UFC Hall dos Anjos vs. Alvarez]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: MacDonald vs. Thompson''''', also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 89''''', was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on June 18, 2016, at [[TD Place Arena]] in [[Ottawa|Ottawa, Ontario, Canada]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2016/3/6/11166574/ufc-rory-macdonald-vs-stephen-thompson-headlines-ufc-fight-night-89-event-ottawa-mma|title=Rory release|date=2016-03-29|access-date=2016-03-29}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the first that the promotion has hosted in [[Ottawa]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/3/4/11160808/ottawa-to-host-first-ufc-event-in-june|title=Ottawa to host first UFC event in June|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2016-03-04|access-date=2016-03-04}} \n\nThe card was headlined by a welterweight bout between former [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] challenger [[Rory MacDonald (fighter)|Rory MacDonald]] and five-time [[kickboxing]] world champion [[Stephen Thompson (fighter)|Stephen Thompson]].\n\nThis event marked the promotion's inaugural fight in the women's flyweight division as former [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] challenger [[Valérie Létourneau]] faced [[Joanne Calderwood]]. Despite that, a formal introduction of the division wasn't made since promotion officials are still determining whether or not to permanently add the division.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/04/joanne-calderwood-meets-valerie-letourneau-in-first-ufc-womens-flyweight-bout-at-ufc-fight-night-89|title=Joanne Calderwood meets Valerie Letourneau in first UFC women's flyweight bout at UFC Fight Night 89|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-04-28|access-date=2016-04-28}}\n\n[[Norifumi Yamamoto]] was scheduled to face [[Chris Beal]] at the event. However, Yamamoto was pulled from the bout on May 26 due to an undisclosed injury and was replaced by former [[List of Bellator champions#Featherweight Championship|Bellator Featherweight Champion]] and one time [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] challenger [[Joe Soto]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/05/kid-yamamoto-scratched-from-ufc-fight-night-89-joe-soto-now-meets-chris-beal|title='Kid' Yamamoto scratched from UFC Fight Night 89, Joe Soto now meets Chris Beal|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-05-26|access-date=2016-05-26}}\n\n[[Alex Garcia (fighter)|Alex Garcia]] was scheduled to face [[Colby Covington]], but pulled out on June 9 due to undisclosed reasons and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Jonathan Meunier]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/06/alex-garcia-out-of-ufc-fight-night-89-bout-with-colby-covington|title=(Updated) Alex Garcia out, Jonathan Meunier in against Colby Covington at UFC Fight Night 89|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-06-09|access-date=2016-06-09}}\n\n[[Randa Markos]] missed weight on her first attempt at the weigh ins, coming in at 117.5 lb. She was given additional time to make the weight limit, but made no attempts to cut further. Instead, she was fined 20 percent of her fight purse, which went to [[Jocelyn Jones-Lybarger]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/06/ufc-fight-night-89-live-and-official-weigh-in-results-1|title=UFC Fight Night 89 official weigh-in results: Headliners on weight, but 1 fighter heavy|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-06-17|access-date=2016-06-18}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Welterweight\n|[[Stephen 23''''') was an installment of the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] (UFC)-produced [[reality television]] series ''[[The Ultimate Fighter]]''.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/01/champ-jedrzejczyk-gadelha-announced-as-tuf-23-coaches-rematch-on-july-8|title=Champ Jedrzejczyk, Gadelha announced as 'TUF 23' coaches, rematch on July following coaching slots on TUF 23|date=2016-01-18|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Jesse Holland|access-date=2016-01-18}}\n\nOn January 17, 2016, during the live broadcast of [[UFC Fight Night 81]], it was announced that the coaches for the season would be [[Joanna Jędrzejczyk]] and [[Cláudia Gadelha]]. The coaches faced each other in a [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] bout at the conclusion of the season.\n\nThe UFC held open tryouts for the show on December 14, 2015. The casting call was for [[Light Heavyweight (MMA)|light heavyweight]], [[Bantamweight (MMA)|women's bantamweight]] and [[Strawweight (MMA)|women's strawweight]] fighters who were at least 21 years old and had a minimum of two wins in three professional fights.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/11/23/9784600/the-ultimate-fighter-23-tryouts-open-for-both-womens-divisions-mens|title='The Ultimate Fighter 23' tryouts open for both women's divisions, men's light heavyweight|date=2015-11-23|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|access-date=2015-11-23}} Subsequently, it was announced that eight light heavyweight and eight women's strawweight competitors would compete on the show. The cast was revealed on April 6, 2016.{{cite web|url=http://www.foxsports.com/watch/the-ultimate-fighter/story/ufc-ultimate-fighter-tuf-23-cast-revealed-joanna-jedrzejczyk-claudia-gadelha-040616|title=Cast revealed for 'The Ultimate Fighter: Team Joanna vs. Team Claudia'|work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2016-04-06|access-date=2016-04-06}}\n\n==Cast==\n\n===Teams===\n{{Col-begin}}\n{{Col-break}}\n;{{Color |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/04/episode-no-1-recap-the-ultimate-fighter-23-team-joanna-vs-team-claudia |title=Episode No. 1 recap: 'The Ultimate Fighter 23: Team Joanna vs. Team show. He introduced them to the coaches, Joanna Jędrzejczyk and Cláudia Gadelha.\n* The preliminary fights began:\n:Women's Strawweight – '''Amanda Cooper''' defeated Mellony Geugjes via submission (armbar) in the first round.\n:Light heavyweight – '''Eric Spicely''' defeated Kenneth Bergh via submission (rear-naked choke) in the first round.\n:Women's Strawweight – '''Lanchana Green''' defeated Ashley Cummins via TKO (knees and punches) in the second round.\n:Light heavyweight – '''Khalil Rountree''' defeated Muhammed Dereese via TKO (punches and soccer kicks to the body) in the second round.\n:Women's Strawweight – '''Jamie Moyle''' defeated Alyssa Krahn via submission (rear-naked choke) in the first round.\n:Light heavyweight – '''Myron Dennis''' defeated Jamelle Jones via TKO (punches) in the first round.\n:Women's Strawweight- '''Ashley Yoder''' defeated Jodie Esquibel via split decision after two rounds.\n:Light heavyweight – '''Elias Urbina IV''' defeated Norman Paraisy via unanimous decision after three rounds.\n:Women's Strawweight – '''JJ Aldrich''' defeated Kristi Lopez via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n:Light heavyweight – '''Abdel Medjedoub''' defeated John Paul Elias via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n:Women's Strawweight – '''Tatiana Suarez''' defeated Chel-c Bailey via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n:Light heavyweight – '''Andrew Sanchez''' defeated Phil Hawes via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n:Women's Strawweight – '''Kate Jackson''' defeated Irene Cabello Rivera via TKO (punches) in the second round.\n:Light heavyweight – '''Cory Hendricks''' defeated Marcel Fortuna via unanimous decision after three rounds.\n:Women's Strawweight – '''Helen Harper''' defeated Amy Montenegro via submission (armbar) in the first round.\n:Light heavyweight – '''Josh Stansbury''' defeated Trevor Carlson via submission (americana) in the first round.\n* White reminds the fighters that the winning fighter and coach will get a [[Harley Davidson]] motorcycle.\n* White kicked off the tournament by flipping a coin (red for Joanna, blue for Cláudia) to determine which team will pick the first fight. Team Cláudia wins the coin toss and opts to choose the first fighter.\n* '''Women's Strawweights selection:'''\n{| class=\"wikitable\"\n|-\n!Coach\n!1st Pick\n!2nd Pick\n!3rd Pick\n!4th Pick\n|-\n!Cláudia\n|Tatiana Suarez\n|Kate Jackson\n|Amanda Cooper\n|Lanchana Green\n|-\n!Joanna\n|JJ Aldrich\n|Jamie Moyle\n|Ashley Yoder\n|Helen Harper\n|}\n* '''Light Heavyweights selection:'''\n{| class=\"wikitable\"\n|-\n!Coach\n!1st Pick\n!2nd Pick\n!3rd Pick\n!4th Pick\n|-\n!Cláudia\n|Andrew Sanchez\n|Eric Spicely\n|Abdel Medjedoub \n|Cory Hendricks\n|-\n!Joanna\n|Josh Stansbury\n|Khalil Rountree\n|Myron Dennis\n|Elias Urbina IV\n|}\n*Jędrzejczyk announces the first fight and matches training partners Rountree against Hendricks.\n\n'''Episode 2: Friends and Foes (April 27, 2016)'''{{cite web|url=http://areyouwatchingthis.com/tv/programs/EP007110800330-The-Ultimate-Fighter-Team-McGregor-vs-Team-Faber-Europe-vs-United-States|title=The Ultimate Fighter – Are You Watching This?!}}\n\n*The fighters make their way into the ''TUF'' house and choose their bedrooms. However, there's a problem when Abdel Medjedoub arrives late and finds out there's only one bed left, which is in one of the female fighters' rooms. Being a Muslim and married, he cannot be put in that situation, so he asks Cory Hendricks, who is single, to room with the women. He takes \"one for the team\" and moves into their room. \n*Hurt from his last fight to get into the house, Hendricks gets his swollen feet checked out by [[orthopedic surgeon]] and even though the bones in his feet are not broken, he suffers from a severe contusions to the soft tissue and must rest up before his next fight with his training partner Khalil Rountree.\n*Things heat up between the coaches Joanna and Cláudia, and a lot of trash talk commences.\n*Helen Harper gets sick and can't train with her team. She is found by Rountree, huddled on the floor in kitchen cupboard with her hands shaking, and is taken to the doctor. \n*'''Cory Hendricks''' defeated Khalil Rountree via submission (rear-naked choke) in the first round.\n*Gadelha announces the first women's fight: Tatiana Suarez vs. JJ Aldrich.\n\n'''Episode 3: Who I Am (May 4, 2016)'''\n*After recovering from being sick, Helen Harper injures her shoulder during grappling training and quickly gets upset, losing mental focus. Once again, Khalil Rountree is there for positive reinforcement. \n*At the ''TUF'' house, Rountree challenges Cory Hendricks to a \"UFC Doughnut Eating Championship\"; who could eat a dozen donuts the fastest in 5 minutes. Rountree is the winner and earns the golden donut while Hendricks has to have one of the women apply makeup and wear a sports bra. Amanda Cooper gives him a full makeover.\n*As a reward for their hard work, the fighters receive a letter from an impressed Dana White inviting them to play a sneak peek of the new [[Doom (2016 video game)|Doom]] video game. \n*Before the fight, UFC welterweight champion [[Robbie Lawler]] and UFC Hall of Famer [[Matt Hughes (fighter)|Matt Hughes]] visit Suarez with words of encouragement.\n*'''Tatiana Suarez''' defeated JJ Aldrich via submission (rear-naked choke) 3 minutes and 14 seconds into the second round. \n*Gadelha announces the next men's fight: [[Andrew Sanchez]] vs. Myron Dennis.\n\n'''Episode 4: Wake Up, Guys! (May 11, 2016)'''\n*After her team goes 0–2 losing the last two fights, Jędrzejczyk, being the extremely competitive person she is, lashes out at her fighters saying they \"train like pussies,\" because she thinks they're slacking off in practice. \n*Team Joanna responds with mixed feelings. When they get back to the ''TUF'' house, Myron Dennis huddles his teammates together and starts a rally cry, chanting what he's going to do to his opponent, [[Andrew Sanchez]]. \n*Cláudia visits the house with her coaches to cook [[Brazilian barbecue]] for the fighters. \n*As a joke, Kahlil Rountree wears a referee shirt and blows a whistle to kick the blue team out of the gym when it was Team Joanna's time to train. But Gadelha and her assistant coaches take their time leaving. Feeling disrespected, Jędrzejczyk gets angry and throws their kicking pads at them. \n*As a way to blow off steam, both teams have a water balloon fight at the house.\n*'''Andrew Sanchez''' defeated Myron Dennis via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n*Gadelha announces the next women's fight: Lanchana Green vs. Helen Harper.\n\n'''Episode 5: Battle of the Brits (May 18, 2016)'''\n\n*Jędrzejczyk brings in former [[Invicta FC|Invicta]] and [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]] strawweight champion, and [[The Ultimate Fighter: A Champion Will Be Crowned|The Ultimate Fighter 20]] winner, [[Carla Esparza]], to help coach her team with wrestling and grappling. \n*Since the fighters cannot have contact with the outside world, ''TUF'' producer Lewis Massoll gets an emergency phone call from Abdel Medjedoub's wife Caroline and allows him to talk to her. Apparently, Medjedoub did something that she was not happy with before the show. She says that she is going to leave him if he does not come back home to [[Montreal]]. \n*'''Lanchana Green''' defeated Helen Harper via unanimous decision after three rounds.\n*After the fight, Team Cláudia goes crazy celebrating the victory and Gadelha shakes hands with Team Joanna's corner. But adding more to the drama, she walks right past Jędrzejczyk herself, which the champion takes this as a huge sign of disrespect to her.\n*Gadelha announces the next men's fight: Eric Spicely vs. Elias Urbina.\n\n'''Episode 6: The Situation (May 25, 2016)'''\n\n*White calls Abdel Medjedoub into his office to discuss his situation about his wife's ultimatum to quit the show or she's going to leave him. White lets him talk to her on the phone but she wants him to answer for his mistake in person. \n*Not wanting Medjedoub chose between his career or his marriage, White gives him a few days to straighten this emergency out and he will fly him back to the show. \n*Elias Urbina shows a few teammates the poster of his ''TUF'' alumni older brother, Hector Urbina, who was a cast member of ''TUF'' season 19.\n*Khalil Rountree's knees have been sore and the doctor says he has minor [[tendinitis]]. During training, he has been getting sharp pain while in different grappling positions and his positive attitude suddenly goes to frustration. So he takes a few days off to rest.\n*At the ''TUF'' house, Rountree and Urbina decide to prank the female fighters by putting baby powder in their hairdryers. In retaliation, the women throw baby powder on some of the men, while they are in the hot tub. Also, Myron Dennis gets a scare from Rountree who jumps out of the tub in the bathroom. \n*As promised, Medjedoub handles his business, flying back in a few days and continues to compete on the show.\n*'''Eric Spicely''' defeated Elias Urbina via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n*Gadelha announces the next women's fight: Amanda Cooper vs. Jamie Moyle.\n\n'''Episode 7: No Easy Fight (June 1, 2016)'''\n\n*Since Team Cláudia could potentially go down as the first team to pull a sweep, Jędrzejczyk is feeling the pressure and will do anything to get her fighters motivated, including saying the upcoming women's match-up with Amanda Cooper will be an \"easy fight\" for Jamie Moyle. Team Cláudia just brushes the comment off.\n*Jędrzejczyk also brings in former UFC bantamweight champion [[T.J. Dillashaw]] to guest coach. \n*At the ''TUF'' house, Khalil Rountree breaks down because he nearly forget his late father's birthday, which he still celebrates in his honor, since he died when Rountree was only 2-year's-old.\n*White surprises the fighters for their hardcore performances this season with a night out on the [[Las Vegas Strip]]. He rents them a [[party bus]] and things get crazy when Ashley Yoder gets on the stripper pole. They then see the show ''[[Absinthe (show)|Absinthe]]'' and things heat up when Josh Stansbury and Myron Dennis are called up on stage to do a strip tease on Jamie Moyle.\n*'''Amanda Cooper''' defeated Jamie Moyle via majority decision after two rounds.\n*After the fight, believing it should have gone to a third round, Jędrzejczyk tells off the judges. Team Joanna is down 0–6.\n*Gadelha announces the next men's fight: Abdel Medjedoub vs. Josh Stansbury.\n\n'''Episode 8: It's a Mental Thing (June 8, 2016)'''\n\n*Team Cláudia remains unbeaten, winning six straight fights and are very close to making ''TUF'' history by becoming the first team ever to sweep the preliminaries. \n*During grappling practice, Cory Hendricks injures his shoulder, causing him to have severe neck and arm pain. So he decides to take it easy before he goes back to hard training again. Instead of going to the doctor, due to fearing that he will be told not to fight, Hendricks sees an [[acupuncturist]], who along with basic needle [[acupuncture]], she performed a therapy called \"[[cupping therapy|cupping]]\" and a Chinese technique called \"[[gua sha]]\". \n*Later, Hendricks has to go to the doctor anyway since his pain continues. The doctor orders an [[MRI]] and has to wait for a diagnoses.\n*'''Josh Stansbury''' defeated Abdel Medjedoub via majority decision after two rounds.\n*The red team finally gets their first win of the season, stopping Team Cláudia's 6–0 win streak.\n*Jędrzejczyk announces the next women's fight: Ashley Yoder vs. Kate Jackson.\n\n'''Episode 9: Beast Inside of You (June 15, 2016)'''\n\n*The doctor visits Hendricks with the results from the MRI. It showed nothing serious, it's just a pain issue, so he can continue in the competition.\n*Since their strawweight title match is coming up soon, Jędrzejczyk and Gadelha have to not only coach, but they have to train as well, and do so at their respected gyms.\n*'''Kate Jackson''' defeated Ashley Yoder via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n*One man and no women from Team Joanna will move on to the semi-finals.\n*Team Cláudia finished 7–1, but ends up in an unfortunate situation that most of them will be fighting against each other. Gadelha states that this hurts her because her team are like a family.\n*White announces the semi-final fights: \n**Andrew Sanchez vs. Eric Spicely.\n**Amanda Cooper vs. Lanchana Green.\n**Tatiana Suarez vs. Kate Jackson.\n**Josh Stansbury vs. Cory Hendricks.\n\n'''Episode 10: Friends Hurting Friends (June 22, 2016)'''\n\n*Team Cláudia still has seven fighters in the competition and has claimed every semifinal in the strawweight division and every light heavyweight, except for one.\n*With a nerve injury in his neck, Cory Hendricks initially decides to push through the pain and continue to train, however, after being in intense constant pain, he takes Cláudia's advice and drops out of the competition to fight in the future.\n*Dana rewards the fighters and arranges to take them out for a night on the town. They feast at [[Brooklyn Bowl|Brooklyn Bowl Buffet]], and the [[High Roller (Ferris wheel)|High Roller]] at [[The Linq]] but Cláudia gets annoyed the Joanna keeps taking the credit for all of Dana's surprises.\n*Khalil Rountree replaces Cory Hendricks in the competition, but it's bittersweet because he has to fight Josh Stansbury, his teammate, and he gets upset.\n*Dana gives Cory another chance after he heals by letting him fight in the ''TUF'' finale.\n*This is the first time that Team Cláudia has to fight each other and they get emotional, especially for Eric Spicely, who breaks down, not wanting to fight [[Andrew Sanchez]], his friend and training partner.\n*It's that time of the season for the Coach's Challenge. This year, it's the biggest and craziest one they've ever done. There are two massive [[dunk tank]]s high off the ground. Both coaches stand at the top and shoot an [[air rifle]] that fires tennis balls at a target. If one of them hits the bullseye of 20 points, the other gets dunked into the ice cold water. The first coach to 100 points wins $10,000 cash, with each fighter getting $1,500 each.\n*Cláudia wins by dunking Joanna 3 times to her 2 and is first to rack up 100 points. So Dana issues one more challenge; see if she can dunk him for another $5,000 and her team getting $500 each. Dana is afraid of heights, but he isn't up there for long since Cláudia hits the bullseye on the first try.\n*'''Andrew Sanchez''' defeated Eric Spicely via knockout (punches) in the first round.\n*After the fight, Andrew is distraught and concerned about knocking out Eric and crushing his dream.\n\n'''Episode 11: Bump in the Road (June 29, 2016) '''\n\n*Cláudia invites UFC bantamweight champion [[Miesha Tate]] to guest coach, helping her team with wrestling techniques.\n*At the ''TUF'' house, the fighters decide to have some fun and put on their own bowling tournament with water bottles as pins. Lanchana Green wins the homemade paper medal. \n*During the weigh-in, Amanda Cooper fails to make weight and weights in at 117 1/2 lb. She has only one hour to lose 1 1/2 pounds. The entire team helps her with the cut and she makes weight.\n*'''Amanda Cooper''' defeated Lanchana Green via submission (rear-naked choke) in the first round.\n\n'''Episode 12: Fight of the Season (July 6, 2016) '''\n*'''Tatiana Suarez''' defeated Kate Jackson via submission ([[Guillotine choke]]) in the first round.\n*'''Khalil Rountree''' defeated Josh Stansbury via TKO ([[Knockout#Technical knockout|Technical Knockout]]) in the first round.\n\n==Tournament Bracket==\n\n===Light heavyweight Bracket===\n{{Round8\n\n\n||{{color box|#CC0000|border=silver}} Myron Dennis|2|{{color Finale==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=The Ultimate Fighter: Team Joanna vs. Team Cláudia Finale\n|image=TUF_23_Finale.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date={{Start date|2016|07|08}}\n|venue=[[MGM Grand Garden Arena]]\n|city= [[Las Vegas Valley|Las Vegas]], [[Nevada]]\n|attendance=8,115{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/07/tuf-23-finale-draws-announced-8115-fans-for-736408-live-gate-in-las-vegas|title=TUF 23 Finale draws announced 8,115 fans for $736,408 live gate in Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-07-09|access-date=2016-07-09}}\n|gate=$736,408\n|previousevent=[[UFC Fight Night: dos Anjos vs. Alvarez]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC 200|UFC 200: Tate vs. Nunes]]\n}}\n'''''The Ultimate Fighter: Team Joanna vs. Team Cláudia Finale''''' (also known as '''''The Ultimate Fighter 23 Finale''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on July 8, 2016 at the [[MGM Grand Garden Arena]] in [[Las Vegas Valley|Las Vegas]], Nevada. This event was a part of UFC International Fight Week.\n\n===Background===\nThe event was headlined by a [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Joanna Jędrzejczyk]] and top contender [[Cláudia Gadelha]]. The pair previously met at [[UFC on Fox: dos Santos vs. Miocic]] in December 2014, with Jędrzejczyk winning via split decision.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2014/12/13/7388609/ufc-on-fox-13-results-jonna-jedrzejczyk-upsets-claudia-gadelha|title=UFC on FOX 13 results: Joanna Jedrzejczyk upsets Claudia Gadelha|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2014-12-13|access-date=2016-04-06}}\n\n[[Scott Askham]] was expected to face [[Anthony Smith (fighter)|Anthony Smith]] at the event, but pulled out on April 28 due to injury and was replaced by [[The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil]] winner [[Cezar Ferreira]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/04/cezar-ferreira-in-for-scott-askham-at-tuf-23-finale-in-las-vegas-vs-anthony-smith|title=Cezar Ferreira in for Scott Askham at TUF 23 Finale in Las Vegas vs. Anthony Smith|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-04-28|access-date=2016-04-28}}\n\n[[Stevie Ray (fighter)|Stevie Ray]] was expected to face [[Jake Matthews (fighter)|Jake Matthews]] at the event, but pulled out on June 2 citing alleged [[visa (document)|visa]] issues and was replaced by [[Kevin Lee]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/6/2/11848330/stevie-ray-out-kevin-lee-in-against-jake-matthews-at-tuf-23-finale|title=Stevie Ray out, Kevin Lee in against Jake Matthews at TUF 23 Finale|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|date=2016-06-02|access-date=2016-06-02}}\n\n[[James Krause (fighter)|James Krause]] was scheduled to face [[The Ultimate Fighter: United States vs. United Kingdom]] lightweight winner [[Ross Pearson]] at the event. However, on June 13, he was pulled from the fight for undisclosed reasons.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/06/james-krause-withdraws-from-the-ultimate-fighter-23-finale-ufc-seeks-new-opponent-for-ross-pearson|title=James Krause withdraws from The Ultimate Fighter 23 Finale, UFC seeks new opponent for Ross Pearson|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2016-06-13|access-date=2016-06-13}} He was replaced by former [[List of Bellator champions#Lightweight Championship|Bellator Lightweight Champion]] and promotional newcomer [[Will Brooks]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/06/will-brooks-signs-with-ufc-meets-ross-pearson-at-the-ultimate-fighter-23-finale|title=Will Brooks signs with UFC, meets Ross Pearson at The Ultimate Fighter 23 Finale|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2016-06-15|access-date=2016-06-15}}\n\n===Results===\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's held on July 23, 2016, at [[United Center]] in [[Chicago, Illinois]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/3/30/11335290/ufc-headed-back-to-chicago-in-july-for-ufc-on-fox-20|title=UFC headed between former [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship|UFC Women's Bantamweight Champion]] [[Holly Holm]] and multiple-time [[Muay Thai]] world champion [[Valentina Shevchenko]].{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Sioux-Falls-and-Chicago-Headliners-Announced|title=Sioux Falls and Chicago headliners announced|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2016-05-08|access-date=2016-05-08}}\n\nA bout between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] challengers [[Anthony Johnson (fighter)|Anthony Johnson]] and [[Glover Teixeira]] was expected to serve as the co-main event. However, Johnson pulled out on June 18 to tend to personal issues.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/06/anthony-johnson-out-of-ufc-on-fox-20-co-headliner-with-glover-teixeira|title=Anthony Johnson out of UFC on FOX 20 co-headliner with Glover Teixeira|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco and John Morgan|date=2016-06-18|access-date=2016-06-18}} In turn, promotion officials elected to pull Teixeira from the card and the pairing was left intact and rescheduled to take place four weeks later at [[UFC 202]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/6/23/12021944/glover-teixeira-out-of-ufc-on-fox-20|title=Glover Teixeira out of UFC on FOX 20|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2016-06-23|access-date=2016-06-23}}{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Johnson-Teixeira-Off-Chicago-Card|title=Johnson-Teixeira off Chicago card|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2016-06-24|access-date=2016-06-24}}{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2016/6/27/12048148/ufc-202-fight-card-anthony-johnson-glover-teixeira-donald-cerrone-las-vegas-mma|title=UFC 202 fight card gets Anthony Johnson vs. Glover Teixeira|author=Thomas Myers|publisher=mmamania.com|date=2016-06-27|access-date=2016-06-27}}\n\n[[Ryan LaFlare]] was expected to face [[Alexander Yakovlev (fighter)|Alexander Yakovlev]] at the event. However, LaFlare was removed from the fight in early June after sustaining an undisclosed injury and was replaced by [[The Ultimate Fighter: American Top Team vs. Blackzilians]] winner [[Kamaru Usman]].{{cite news|url=http://www.redeyechicago.com/sports/redeye-ufc-announces-injury-replacement-for-july-23-card-at-united-center-20160603-story.html|title='Nigerian Nightmare' lands spot for UFC event at United Center|publisher=redeyechicago.com|author=Chris Sosa|date=2016-06-03|access-date=2016-06-07}}\n\n[[Anthony Rocco Martin]] was expected to face [[Michel Prazeres]] at the event. However, Martin pulled out on July 6 due to a neck injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer J.C. Cottrell.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/07/newcomer-j-c-cottrell-in-for-martin-vs-michel-prazeres-at-ufc-on-fox-20-in-chicago|title=Newcomer J.C. Urbina still weighed in as a potential backup opponent should there be a need for him to compete in another bout. Since that did not materialize, he will be re-booked for another card in the near future.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/07/george-sullivan-pulled-from-ufc-on-fox-for-potential-compliance-issue-with-anti-doping-policy-1|title=George Sullivan pulled from UFC on FOX 20 for 'potential compliance issue' with anti-doping policy|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-07-21|access-date=2016-07-21}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Bantamweight\n|[[Valentina Shevchenko]]\n|def.\n|[[Holly Holm]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (49–46, 49–46, 49–46)\n|5\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Edson Barboza]]\n|def.\n|[[Gilbert Melendez]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (30–27, 30–27, 29–28)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Francis Ngannou]]\n|def.\n|Bojan Mihajlović\n|TKO (punches)\n|1\n|1:34\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Strawweight\n|[[Felice Herrig]]\n|def.\n|[[Kailin Curran]]\n|Submission (rear-naked choke)\n|1\n|1:59\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent card|Preliminary Card (Fox)|header=no}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Bantamweight\n|[[Eddie Wineland]]\n|def.\n|[[Frankie Saenz]]\n|TKO (punches)\n|3\n|1:54\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|[[Darren Elkins]]\n|def.\n|[[Godofredo Pepey]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (29–27, 29–27, 30–26)\n|3\n|5:00\n|Pepey was deducted 1 point in round 2 due to an illegal knee.\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Welterweight\n|[[Kamaru Usman]]\n|def.\n|[[Alexander Yakovlev (fighter)|Alexander Yakovlev]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (30–25, 30–25, 30–25)\n|3\n|5:00\n|Yakovlev was deducted 1 point in round 1 due to grabbing the cage.\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Michel Prazeres]]\n|def.\n|J.C. culminating with [[UFC 200]]. It also marked the second North American card to air live exclusively on their subscription-based digital network, UFC Fight Pass, and the first men's championship fight broadcast over that medium.\n\nThe event was headlined by a [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] bout between then-champion [[Rafael dos Anjos]] and former two-time [[List of Bellator champions#Lightweight Championship|Bellator Lightweight Champion]] [[Eddie Alvarez]].\n\nA lightweight bout between [[John Makdessi]] and [[Mehdi Baghdad]], originally scheduled for [[UFC 199]], was moved to this event possibly to bolster the card.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/04/mehdi-baghdad-vs-john-makdessi-moves-from-ufc-199-in-june-to-ufc-fight-night-90-in-july|title=Mehdi Baghdad vs. John Makdessi Muhammad]].{{cite news|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Alan-Jouban-Gets-New-Opponent-Belal-Muhammad-for-UFC-Fight-Night-on-July-7-106175|title=Alan Jouban gets new opponent Belal Muhammad for UFC Gerbasi|date=2016-04-16|access-date=2016-04-16}}\n\n==Background==\nThis was the first UFC event under the ownership of a group led by [[Endeavor (company)|Endeavor]] which included [[Silver Lake Partners]], [[Kohlberg Kravis Roberts]], and [[MSD Capital]]; their purchase of [[Zuffa]] was announced two days earlier.{{cite web|url=https://www.nytimes.com/2016/07/11/business/dealbook/ufc-sells-itself-for-4-billion.html|title=U.F.C. Sells Itself for $4 Billion|work=[[The New York Times]]|last=de la Merced|first=Michael|date=July 11, 2016|access-date=October 8, 2019}} The event also was the first that the organization has hosted in [[South Dakota]].{{cite web|url=http://www.argusleader.com/story/sports/2016/07/14/area-fans-get-taste-ufc-sioux-falls/87024774/|title=Area fans get taste of UFC in Sioux [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Lesnar vs. Team dos Santos]] winner [[Tony Ferguson]] was expected to face [[The Ultimate Fighter: Live]] winner [[Michael Chiesa]].{{cite Gerbasi|date=2016-05-08|access-date=2016-05-08}} However, Chiesa pulled out due to a back injury on June 27.{{cite news|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Michael-Chiesa-Injured-Out-of-July-13-UFC-Fight-Night-Headliner-with-Tony-Ferguson-107037|title=Michael Chiesa injured, out of July 13 UFC Fight Night headliner with Tony Ferguson|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Staff|date=2016-06-27|access-date=2016-06-27}} On the following day, it was announced that promotional newcomer [[Lando Vannata]] would replace him and the bout would be moved to the co-main event, as a bantamweight bout between former interim [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] challenger [[Michael McDonald (fighter)|Michael McDonald]] and [[John Lineker]] was revealed as the new headliner.{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/McDonald-Lineker-to-Headline-Sioux-Falls-Event-on-July-13|title=McDonald-Lineker to headline Sioux Falls event on July 13|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|access-date=2016-06-28|date=2016-06-28}}\n\n[[Alex White (fighter)|Alex White]] was expected to face [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team McGregor vs. Team Faber]] winner [[Ryan Hall (grappler)|Ryan Hall]], but pulled out on June 28 due to an undisclosed injury. |title=Archived copy |access-date=2016-09-08 |archive-date=2011-06-27 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20110627142203/http://mmapayout.com/blue-book/pay-per-view/ |url-status=dead }}\n|previousevent=[[UFC on Fox: Holm vs. Shevchenko]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Rodríguez vs. Caceres]]\n}}\n'''''UFC 201: Lawler vs. Woodley''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on July 30, 2016, at [[State Farm Arena|Philips Arena]] in [[Atlanta, Georgia]].{{cite news|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Robbie-Lawler-vs-Tyron-Woodley-Targeted-for-UFC-201-Headliner-in-Atlanta-104485|title=Robbie Lawler vs. Tyron Woodley targeted for UFC 201 headliner in Atlanta|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2016-05-04|access-date=2016-05-04}}\n\n==Background==\nThis was the third event that the organization has hosted in [[Atlanta]], following [[UFC 88]] in September 2008 and [[UFC 145]] in April 2012.\n\nThe event was headlined by a [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Robbie Lawler]] and [[Tyron Woodley]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/05/robbie-lawler-vs-tyron-woodley-official-for-ufc-201-headliner-in-atlanta|title=Robbie Lawler vs. Tyron Woodley official for UFC 201 headliner in Atlanta|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-05-17|access-date=2016-05-17}}\n\nA [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Demetrious Johnson]] and [[Wilson Reis]] was expected to serve as the co-main event.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/05/demetrious-johnson-to-defend-flyweight-title-against-wilson-reis-at-ufc-201|title=Demetrious Johnson to defend flyweight title against Wilson Reis at UFC 201|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-05-18|access-date=2016-05-18}} However, on July 8, it was announced that Johnson pulled out due to an undisclosed injury and the bout was rescheduled for [[UFC on Fox 24]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/07/flyweight-champ-demetrious-johnson-injured-out-of-ufc-201-title-fight-with-wilson-reis|title=Flyweight champ Demetrious Johnson injured, out of UFC 201 title fight with Wilson Reis|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2016-07-08|access-date=2016-07-08}} Reis was expected to remain on the card against promotional newcomer Sean Santella,{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/07/promotional-newcomer-sean-santella-steps-in-to-fight-wilson-reis-at-ufc-201-in-atlanta|title=Promotional newcomer Sean Santella steps in to fight Wilson Reis at UFC 201 in Atlanta|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-07-13|access-date=2016-07-13}} but a few days later Santella announced the bout was scrapped because the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]] said \"he needed to get more medicals done and there wasn't enough time\".{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/07/sean-santella-says-short-notice-ufc-201-bout-against-wilson-reis-has-been-scrapped|title=Sean Santella says short-notice UFC 201 bout against Wilson Reis has been scrapped|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-07-16|access-date=2016-07-16}} In turn, Reis faced [[Hector Sandoval]], another newcomer.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmanews.com/team-alpha-males-hector-sandoval-takes-on-wilson-reis-at-ufc-201/|title=Team Alpha Male's Hector Sandoval takes on Wilson Reis at UFC 201|publisher=mmanews.com|author=Dana Becker|date=2016-07-18|access-date=2016-07-18}}\n\n[[Cláudio Silva]] was expected to face [[Siyar Bahadurzada]] at the event. However, on June 16, Silva pulled out due to injury and was replaced by [[Jorge Masvidal]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/06/jorge-masvidal-replaces-claudio-silva-meets-siyar-bahadurzada-at-ufc-201jorge-masvidal-replaces-claudio-silva-meets-siyar-bahadurzada-at-ufc-201|title=Jorge Masvidal replaces Claudio Silva, meets Siyar Bahadurzada at UFC 201|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-06-16|access-date=2016-06-17}} In turn, Bahadurzada pulled out of the bout on July 12 citing an illness and was replaced by [[The Ultimate Fighter: United States vs. United Kingdom]] winner [[Ross Pearson]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/07/illness-forces-siyar-bahadurzada-out-of-ufc-201-ufc-seeks-opponent-for-jorge-masvidal|title=Illness forces Siyar Bahadurzada out of UFC 201, UFC seeks opponent for Jorge Masvidal|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-07-12|access-date=2016-07-12}}{{cite news|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/17053115/ross-pearson-agrees-fight-jorge-masvidal-ufc-201|title=Ross Pearson agrees to fight Jorge Masvidal at UFC 201|publisher=[[ESPN]]|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2016-07-13|access-date=2016-07-13}}\n\n[[Ray Borg]] was expected to face [[Fredy Serrano]] at the event, but pulled out on July 21 due to injury and was replaced by [[Ryan Benoit]].{{cite news|url=http://combatpress.com/2016/07/ray-borg-injured-fredy-serrano-now-meets-ryan-benoit-at-ufc-201/|title=Ray Borg injured, Fredy Serrano now meets Ryan Benoit at UFC 201|publisher=combatpress.com|author=Rob Tatum|date=2016-07-21|access-date=2016-07-29}}\n\n[[Justin Scoggins]] was expected to face [[Ian McCall (fighter)|Ian McCall]] in a [[Flyweight (MMA)|flyweight]] bout. However, two days before the event, Scoggins announced he was struggling during the weight cut and was not going to make the contracted weight. The [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]] pulled Scoggins and canceled the bout and McCall still weighed in as an alternate. Scoggins announced he will move to [[Bantamweight (MMA)|bantamweight]] for his next fight.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/07/weight-cutting-issues-force-justin-scoggins-out-of-ufc-201-flyweight-fight-vs-ian-mccall|title=Video: Weight-cutting issues force Justin Scoggins out of UFC 201 vs. McCall (updated)|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-07-28|access-date=2016-07-28}} As a result, McCall reportedly received his fight purse and a \"win\" bonus.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/07/despite-ufc-201-bout-cancellation-ian-mccall-gets-fight-purse-and-win-bonus|title=Despite UFC 201 bout cancellation, Ian McCall gets fight purse and win bonus|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Dann Stupp and John Morgan|date=2016-07-30|access-date=2016-07-30}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card}}\n{{MMAevent Condit]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 202: Diaz vs. McGregor 2''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that was held on August 20, 2016, at the [[T-Mobile Arena]] in [[Paradise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas metropolitan area]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/06/rematch-between-nate-diaz-and-conor-mcgregor-set-for-ufc-202|title=Rematch between Nate Diaz and Conor McGregor set for UFC 202|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Brent Brookhouse|date=2016-06-04|access-date=2016-06-04}}\n\nThis event was the first one under the full control of the new ownership group consisting of [[William Morris Endeavor]], [[Silver Lake Partners]], [[Kohlberg Kravis Roberts]] and [[MSD Capital]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/8/20/12565212/ufc-completes-sale-to-wme-img|title=UFC completes sale to WME-IMG|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Meltzer|date=2016-08-20|access-date=2016-08-20}}\n\nThis event was the most bought UFC pay-per-view of all time with a buyrate of 1.65 million, until [[UFC 229]] (which also featured McGregor in the main event) broke the record with 2.4 million.{{cite web |last1=Simon |first1=Zane |title=Report - UFC 229: Khabib vs. McGregor hits new record high of 2.4 million PPV buys |url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2018/10/11/17964814/ufc-229-khabib-nurmagomedov-vs-conor-mcgregor-ppv-buy-record-mma-two-half-million-new-high-mma-news |website=Bloody Elbow |date=11 October 2018 |access-date=6 February 2021 |archive-date=5 October 2020 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20201005155825/https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2018/10/11/17964814/ufc-229-khabib-nurmagomedov-vs-conor-mcgregor-ppv-buy-record-mma-two-half-million-new-high-mma-news |url-status=dead }}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was headlined by a welterweight rematch between [[The Ultimate Fighter 5]] winner and former lightweight title challenger [[Nate Diaz]] and [[UFC Featherweight Championship|UFC Featherweight Champion]] [[Conor McGregor]].\n\n[[File:Conor McGregor 2016.png|thumb|left|190px|UFC President [[Dana White]] said they worked on booking the rematch due to \"McGregor's obsession with it\".{{cite web|url=http://www.flocombat.com/article/42361-ufc-still-trying-to-land-diaz-rematch-for-obsessed-mcgregor|title=UFC still trying to land Diaz rematch for \"obsessed\" McGregor|publisher=flocombat.com|author=Jeremy Botter|date=2016-06-02|access-date=2016-07-31}}]]\n\nThe pairing previously met earlier in the year at [[UFC 196]]. McGregor was expected to challenge then [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Rafael dos Anjos]], but Dos Anjos pulled out due to a [[broken foot]] only 11 days before the event. Diaz eventually replaced him and the bout was shifted to the welterweight division.{{cite news|url=http://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/ufc-196-conor-mcgregor-cm-punk-new-main-event-022316|title= Diaz gets the call, faces Conor McGregor in new UFC 196 main event|work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2016-02-23|access-date=2016-03-29}} Diaz won the fight via rear-naked choke in the second round.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/03/ufc-196-results-cm-punk-shocks-conor-mcgregor-with-second-round-choke|title=UFC 196 results: Diaz shocks Conor McGregor with second-round choke|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2016-03-06|access-date=2016-03-06}}{{Dead link|date=July 2025 |bot=InternetArchiveBot |fix-attempted=yes }} Their rematch was originally expected to take place at [[UFC 200]], but everything changed during mid-April, as a disagreement between McGregor and the UFC, regarding his media schedule, led to his removal from the event and then Diaz was also pulled out.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/4/19/11464766/dana-white-conor-mcgregor-pulled-from-ufc-200-because-he-wouldnt|title=Dana White: Conor McGregor pulled from UFC 200, because he wouldn't attend press conference|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-04-19|access-date=2016-06-04}}\n\nA bout between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] challengers [[Anthony Johnson (fighter)|Anthony Johnson]] and [[Glover Teixeira]] was expected to serve as the co-main event for [[UFC on Fox: Holm vs. Shevchenko]]. However, Johnson pulled out one month before the event to tend to personal issues.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/06/anthony-johnson-out-of-ufc-on-fox-20-co-headliner-with-glover-teixeira|title=Anthony Johnson out of UFC on FOX 20 co-headliner with Glover Teixeira|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco and John Morgan|date=2016-06-18|access-date=2016-06-18}} In turn, promotion officials elected to pull Teixeira from the card and the pairing was left intact and rescheduled to take place at this event.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2016/6/27/12048148/ufc-202-fight-card-anthony-johnson-glover-teixeira-donald-cerrone-las-vegas-mma|title=UFC 202 fight card gets Anthony Johnson vs. Glover Teixeira|author=Thomas Myers|publisher=mmamania.com|date=2016-06-27|access-date=2016-06-27}}\n\n[[Dong Hyun Kim]] was expected to face [[Neil Magny]] at the event. However, Kim pulled out due to injury on July 12 and was replaced by [[Lorenz Larkin]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/07/dong-hyun-kim-out-of-ufc-202-bout-against-neil-magny-promotion-seeking-replacement|title=Dong Hyun Kim out of UFC 202 bout against Neil Magny, promotion seeking replacement|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-07-12|access-date=2016-07-12}}{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/7/13/12175050/ufc-202-fight-card-lorenz-larkin-steps-in-against-neil-magny|title=Lorenz Larkin steps in against Neil Magny at UFC 202|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|date=2016-07-13|access-date=2016-07-13}}\n\nA welterweight contest between former [[List of World Extreme Cagefighting champions#Welterweight Championship|WEC Welterweight Champion]] and former interim [[UFC Welterweight Championship|UFC Welterweight Champion]] [[Carlos Condit]] and [[Demian Maia]], a former [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] challenger, was scheduled to take place at this event, but was moved a week later to headline an already scheduled [[UFC on Fox: Maia vs. Condit|FOX event]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/7/19/12231530/carlos-condit-vs-demian-maia-moved-from-ufc-202-to-ufc-on-fox-21-main|title=Carlos Condit vs. Demian Maia moved from UFC 202 to UFC on FOX 21 main event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-07-19|access-date=2016-07-19}}\n\n[[Sultan Aliev]] was expected to face [[Lim Hyun-gyu]] at the event. However, Aliev pulled out of the fight in early August citing a wrist injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/08/sultan-aliev-injured-out-of-ufc-202-bout-with-hyun-gyu-lim|title=Sultan Aliev injured, out of UFC 202 bout with Hyun Gyu Lim|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-08-03|access-date=2016-08-03}} Lim faced promotional newcomer [[Mike Perry (fighter)|Mike Perry]].{{cite web|url=http://www.fightsports.tv/story/32686695/mike-perry-replaces-aliev-at-ufc-202|title=Mike Perry replaces Aliev at UFC 202|publisher=fightsports.tv|author=Staff|date=2016-08-04|access-date=2016-08-04|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160822170653/http://www.fightsports.tv/story/32686695/mike-perry-replaces-aliev-at-ufc-202|archive-date=2016-08-22}}\n\nOn August 5, [[Sean Strickland]] pulled out of his planned bout against [[Tim Means]] due to a knee injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/08/sean-strickland-out-at-ufc-202-promotion-seeks-new-opponent-for-tim-means|title=Sean Strickland out at UFC 202; promotion seeks new opponent for Tim Means|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2016-08-05|access-date=2016-08-05}} He was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Sabah Homasi]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/8/9/12414486/sabah-homasi-steps-in-to-face-tim-means-at-ufc-202eans|title=Sabah Homasi steps in to face Tim Means at UFC 202|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-08-09|access-date=2016-08-09}}\n\n[[The Ultimate Fighter: China]] winner [[Ning Guangyou]] tested positive for [[clenbuterol]] from an out-of-competition sample taken May 19, but [[USADA]] found he ingested the substance without fault or negligence. They reviewed the evidence of the case – Ning's whereabouts, dietary habits and the lab reports showing \"very low parts per billion concentrations\" of the substance – and determined that it very likely came from tainted meat, as countries like Mexico and China have a high risk for meats contaminated with the substance. Ning will not face a suspension. He was expected to face [[Marlon Vera]] at this event, but due to this incident the bout was pushed back one week for [[UFC on Fox: Maia vs. Condit]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/8/9/12416676/ufc-s-ning-guangyou-fails-drug-test-for-clenbuterol-usada-finds-no|title=UFC's Ning Guangyou fails drug test for clenbuterol, USADA finds no '''Anthony Johnson''' and '''Donald Cerrone'''\n\n==Reported payout==\nThe following is the reported payout to the fighters as reported to the [[Nevada Athletic Commission|Nevada State Athletic Commission]]. It does not include sponsor money and also does not include the UFC's traditional \"fight night\" bonuses. The total disclosed payout for the event was $6,106,000.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/08/full-ufc-202-salaries-mcgregor-diaz-get-5-million-of-6-1-million-total-payout|title=Full UFC 202 salaries: McGregor, Diaz get $5 million of $6.1 million total payout|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-08-21|access-date=2016-08-21}} \n* '''Conor McGregor:''' $3,000,000 (no win bonus) def. Nate Diaz: $2,000,000\n* '''Anthony Johnson:''' $270,000 (includes $135,000 win bonus) def. Glover Teixeira: $65,000\n* '''Donald Cerrone:''' $170,000 (includes $85,000 win bonus) def. Rick Story: $41,000\n* '''Mike Perry:''' $20,000 (includes $10,000 win bonus) def. Hyun Gyu Lim: $18,000\n* '''Tim Means:''' $62,000 (includes $31,000 win bonus) def. Sabah Homasi: $12,000\n* '''Cody Garbrandt:''' $54,000 (includes $27,000 win bonus) def. Takeya Mizugaki: $39,000\n* '''Raquel Pennington:''' $46,000 (includes $23,000 win bonus) def. Elizabeth Phillips: $12,000\n* '''Artem Lobov:''' $26,000 (includes $13,000 win bonus) def. Chris Avila: $10,000\n* '''Cortney Casey:''' $40,000 (includes $20,000 win bonus) def. Randa Markos: $14,000\n* '''Lorenz Larkin:''' $78,000 (includes $39,000 win bonus) def. Neil Magny: $47,000\n* '''Colby Covington:''' $42,000 (includes $21,000 win bonus) def. Max Griffin: $10,000\n* '''Marvin Vettori:''' $20,000 (includes $10,000 win bonus) def. Alberto Uda: $10,000\n\n==Records set==\nMcGregor's fight purse was the highest in history, breaking [[Brock Lesnar]]'s record from [[UFC 200]] by $500,000. Also, this event estimates doing 1.65 million buys on pay-per-view, putting it slightly ahead of the previous record (1.6 million) from [[UFC 100]], which featured the second bout between [[Brock Lesnar]] and [[Frank Mir]].\n\n==See also==\n*[[List of UFC events]]\n*[[2016 in UFC]]\n\n==References==\n{{Reflist}}\n{{UFC Events}}\n\n[[Category:Ultimate Fighting Championship flagship Miocic vs. Overeem\n|image=UFC 203 event poster.jpg\n|promotion=Ultimate Fighting Championship\n|date={{start date|2016|09|10}}\n|venue=[[Quicken Loans Arena]]\n|city=[[Cleveland, Ohio]]\n|attendance=18,875{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/09/ufc-203-draws-announced-sold-out-attendance-of-18875-for-2-6-million-live-gate|title=UFC 203 draws announced sold-out attendance news|url=http://mmapayout.com/2016/09/ufc-203-ppv-buys-estimated-between-425k-475k/|title=UFC 203 PPV buys estimated between 425K-475K|publisher=mmapayout.com|author=Jason Overeem''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on September 10, 2016, at the [[Quicken Loans Arena]] in [[Cleveland, Ohio]].{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Miocic-Overeem-Headlines-UFC-203-in-Cleveland|title=Miocic-Overeem headlines UFC 203 in Cleveland|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2016-05-25|access-date=2016-05-25}}\n\n==Background==\nThis was the first UFC event in Cleveland.\n\nThe event was headlined by a [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]] bout between current champion (and Cleveland area native) [[Stipe Miocic]] against [[K-1 World Grand Prix 2010 Final|2010 K-1 World Grand Prix]] winner and former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Heavyweight Championship|Strikeforce Heavyweight Champion]] [[Alistair Overeem]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/5/25/11779536/stipe-miocic-vs-alistair-overeem-set-for-ufc-203-in-clevelander|title=Stipe Miocic vs. Alistair Overeem set for UFC 203 in Cleveland|author=Danny Segura|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2016-05-25|access-date=2016-05-25}}\n\nThe professional debut of former [[Professional wrestling|professional wrestler]] [[CM Punk]] took place at this event. Punk, who signed with the UFC in December 2014, had a delayed debut due to past injuries from professional wrestling that needed treatment and also due to his need for training time. He made his debut against [[Mickey Gall]] in a welterweight bout.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/06/cm-punk-slated-for-debut-vs-mickey-gall-at-ufc-203-in-september|title=CM Punk slated for debut vs. Mickey Gall at UFC 203 in September|author=Brent Brookhouse|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2016-06-23|access-date=2016-06-23}}\n\n[[File:CM Punk on ring apron.jpg|thumb|left|205px|The signing of [[CM Punk]] was originally announced during the [[UFC 181]] broadcast on December 6, 2014.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/12/former-wwe-wrestler-phil-cm-punk-brooks-signs-with-ufc-for-expected-2015-debut|title=Former WWE wrestler Phil 'CM Punk' Brooks signs with UFC for expected 2015 debut|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2014-12-07|access-date=2016-08-12}}]]\n\n[[Ben Rothwell]] was expected to face former heavyweight champion [[Fabrício Werdum]] at this event. However, on August 11, Rothwell pulled out due to injury. A team representative said he may have suffered a torn [[meniscus (anatomy)|meniscus]] and could have an [[anterior cruciate ligament injury|ACL injury]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/08/injury-forces-ben-rothwell-out-of-ufc-203-co-main-event-with-fabricio-werdum|title=Injury forces Ben Rothwell out of UFC 203 co-main event with Fabrício Werdum|author=Steven Marrocco|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2016-08-11|access-date=2016-08-11}} He was replaced by [[Travis Browne]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/08/travis-browne-steps-in-for-ben-rothwell-rematches-fabricio-werdum-at-ufc-203|title=Travis Browne steps in for Ben Rothwell, rematches Fabricio Werdum at UFC 203|author=Steven Marrocco and John Morgan|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2016-08-11|access-date=2016-08-11}} This was a rematch as Werdum defeated Browne via unanimous decision at [[UFC on Fox: Werdum vs. Browne]] in 2014.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/ufc-on-fox-11-results-photos-fabricio-werdum-deominates-travis-browne-in-upset|title=UFC on FOX 11 results, photos: Fabricio Werdum dominates Travis Browne in upset|author=Matt Erickson|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2014-04-19|access-date=2016-08-11}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Erik Koch]] and [[Drew Dober]] was originally booked for [[UFC 195]], but Koch pulled out due to an injury and the bout was scrapped.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/12/scott-holtzman-replaces-erik-koch-at-ufc-195|title=Scott Holtzman replaces Erik Koch at UFC 195|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-12-04|access-date=2016-08-11}} The fight was later rescheduled for this event, until Koch pulled out again due to injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Jason Gonzalez (fighter)|Jason Gonzalez]].{{Cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/6/29/12062278/erik-koch-vs-drew-dober-added-to-ufc-203|title=Erik Koch vs. Drew Dober added to UFC 203|author=Danny Segura|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2016-06-29|access-date=2016-06-29}}{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/08/erik-koch-out-drew-dober-draws-newcomer-jason-gonzalez-at-ufc-203|title=Erik Koch out, Drew Dober draws newcomer Jason Gonzalez at UFC 203|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-08-11|access-date=2016-08-11}}\n\n[[Mairbek Taisumov]] was expected to face [[Nik Lentz]] at the event. However, Taisumov was removed from the fight on September 1 for alleged [[visa (document)|visa]] issues and replaced by promotional newcomer [[Michael McBride (fighter)|Michael McBride]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/9/1/12757134/nik-lentz-gets-late-replacement-michael-mcbride-at-ufc-203|title=Nik Lentz gets late replacement Michael McBride at UFC 203|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2016-09-01|access-date=2016-09-01}}\n\nOn September 5, it was announced that the lightweight bout between [[Damir Hadžović]] and Yusuke Kasuya, slated to open the preliminary portion of the card, was postponed and will now take place at October's [[UFC Fight Night: Lamas vs. Penn]] after Hadžović experienced issues with his travel [[visa (document)|visa]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/09/saturdays-ufc-203-event-opener-moves-to-ufc-fight-night-97-in-philippines|title=Saturday's UFC 203 event-opener moves to UFC Fight Night 97 in Philippines|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-09-05|access-date=2016-09-05|archive-date=2016-09-05|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160905235149/http://mmajunkie.com/2016/09/saturdays-ufc-203-event-opener-moves-to-ufc-fight-night-97-in-philippines|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Ray Borg]] was scheduled to face [[Ian McCall (fighter)|Ian McCall]] at the event. However, Borg pulled out of the fight on September 7 citing an illness. With no time to find a suitable replacement, McCall was removed from the card.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/09/lightning-strikes-again-for-ian-mccall-fight-with-ray-borg-pulled-from-saturdays-ufc-203|title=Lightning strikes again for Ian McCall: Fight with Ray Borg pulled from Saturday's UFC 203|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-09-08|access-date=2016-09-08}}\n\nMichael McBride missed weight, coming in at 158 lb. As a result, he was fined 20% of his fight purse, which went to his opponent Nik Lentz.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/09/ufc-203-early-and-official-weigh-in-results-9-a-m-et-1-1|title=UFC 203 early weigh-in results: How much is Alistair Overeem being fined for being late to the scale?|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-09-09|access-date=2016-09-09}}\n\nOn the day of the event, [[C.B. Dollaway]] was forced out of his bout against [[Francimar Barroso]] due to a back injury he sustained in a malfunctioning elevator at his hotel. As a result, Barroso was also removed from the event.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/9/10/12871250/elevator-incident-forces-c-b-dollaway-out-of-ufc-203|title=Elevator incident forces C.B. event\n|name=UFC Fight Night: Arlovski vs. Barnett\n|image=UFC_Hamburg.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date= {{start date|2016|09|03}}\n|venue= [[Barclaycard Arena
