news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/09/ufc-fight-night-93-draws-11763-fans-for-live-gate-of-913428-in-hamburg-germany|title=UFC Fight Night 93 draws 11,763 fans for live gate of $913,428 in Hamburg, Germany|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-09-03|access-date=2016-09-03}}\n|gate=$913,428\n|previousevent=[[UFC on Fox: Maia vs. Condit]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC 203|UFC 203: Miocic vs. Overeem]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Arlovski vs. Barnett''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 93''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on September 3, 2016, at [[Barclaycard Arena (Hamburg)|Barclaycard Arena]] in [[Hamburg, Germany]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/5/7/11616352/ufc-announces-sept-3-fight-night-in-hamburg-germany|title=UFC announces Fight Night event in Hamburg, Germany|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Meltzer|date=2016-05-07|access-date=2016-05-07}}\n\n==Background==\nAfter holding four previous events in Germany ([[Cologne]], [[Oberhausen]] and [[Berlin]] twice), the event was the first that the organization has hosted in [[Hamburg]].\n\nA heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight Champions]] [[Andrei Arlovski]] and [[Josh Barnett]] served as the event headliner.{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Arlovski-Barnett-Headlines-Hamburg-Event|title=Arlovski-Barnett headlines Hamburg event|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2016-05-30|access-date=2016-05-30}}{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/05/andrei-arlovski-vs-josh-barnett-headlines-ufc-fight-night-in-hamburg|title=Andrei Arlovski vs. Josh Barnett headlines UFC Fight Night in Hamburg|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-05-30|access-date=2016-05-30}}\n\nPromotional newcomer [[Emil Weber Meek]] was expected to face fellow newcomer [[Jessin Ayari]] at the event. However, Meek was removed from the card on July 20 due to \"compliance\" issues, related to the UFC’s Anti-Doping Policy.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/07/emil-weber-meek-off-ufc-fight-night-93-card-due-to-potential-compliance-issue|title=Emil Weber Meek off UFC Fight Night 93 card due to potential compliance issue|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-07-20|access-date=2016-07-20}} One week later, he was replaced by another newcomer [[Jim Wallhead]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/7/27/12295086/jim-wallhead-vs-jessin-ayari-booked-for-ufc-fight-night-hamburg|title=Jim Wallhead vs. Jessin Ayari booked for UFC Fight Night Hamburg|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2016-07-27|access-date=2016-07-27}}\n\n[[Reza Madadi]] was expected to face [[Rustam Khabilov]] at the event. However on July 25, Madadi pulled out due to undisclosed reasons and was replaced by [[Leandro Silva (MMA)|Leandro Silva]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/07/reza-madadi-off-ufc-fight-night-93-rustam-khabilov-now-meets-leandro-silva|title=Reza Madadi off UFC Fight Night 93; Rustam Khabilov now meets Leandro Silva|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-07-25|access-date=2016-07-25}}\n\nA long rumored fight between [[Henry Briones]] and [[Brad Pickett]] was expected to take place on the card. However, Briones was replaced in early August for undisclosed reasons by [[Iuri Alcântara]].{{Cite web|url=http://sportv.globo.com/site/combate/noticia/2016/08/iuri-marajo-substitui-briones-e-encara-brad-pickett-no-ufc-da-alemanha.html|title=Iuri Marajo replaces Briones and sees Brad Pickett at UFC in Germany (Brazilian Portuguese)|publisher=sportv.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|date=2016-08-02|access-date=2016-08-02}} Subsequently, that pairing was rescheduled to take place a month later at [[UFC 204]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/8/9/12419480/brad-pickett-vs-iuri-alcantara-scheduled-for-ufc-204-in-manchester|title=Brad Pickett vs. Iuri Alcantara scheduled for UFC 204|author=Danny Segura|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2016-08-10|access-date=2016-08-10}}\n\n[[Aisling Daly]] was expected to face former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Atomweight Championship|Invicta FC Atomweight Champion]] [[Michelle Waterson]] at the event. However, Daly was pulled from the fight on August 9 due to injury. Promotion officials reportedly searched to replace Daly in an attempt to keep Waterson on the card.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/08/aisling-daly-out-at-ufc-fight-night-93-promotion-seeks-new-opponent-for-michelle-waterson|title=Aisling Daly out at UFC Fight Night 93, promotion seeks new opponent for Michelle Waterson|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-08-09|access-date=2016-08-09}} In turn, Waterson was removed from the card after injuring her finger.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmanews.com/michelle-waterson-now-out-of-ufc-fight-night-93-with-injury/|title=Michelle Waterson now out of UFC Fight Night 93 with injury|publisher=mmanews.com|author=Dana Becker|date=2016-08-15|access-date=2016-08-15}}\n\n[[Germaine de Randamie]] was scheduled to face [[Ashlee Evans-Smith]] on the card. However, de Randamie pulled out of the fight in mid-August citing a foot injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Veronica Macedo]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/08/veronica-macedo-replaces-germaine-de-randamie-at-ufc-fight-night-93-in-germany|title=Veronica Macedo replaces Germaine de Randamie at UFC Fight Night 93 in event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on August 6, 2016, at [[Vivint Smart Home Arena]] in [[Salt Lake City, Utah]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-Visits-Salt-Lake-City-on-Aug-6|title=UFC visits Salt Lake City on August 6|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2016-06-09|access-date=2016-06-09}}{{cite 6|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-06-09|access-date=2016-06-09}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the first that the organization has hosted in [[Utah]].\n\nThe promotion previously scheduled an event for Salt Lake City in August 2010 for [[UFC Live: Jones vs. Matyushenko]]. However, the event was moved to [[San Diego, California]] due to lower than anticipated ticket sales in the Utah market.{{cite web|url=http://lasvegassun.com/news/2010/jun/14/ufc-moves-event-salt-lake-city-san-diego-due-lack-/|title=UFC moves event from Salt Lake City to San Diego for lack of ticket sales|publisher=lasvegassun.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2010-06-14|access-date=2016-06-09}}\n\nA featherweight bout between [[The Ultimate Fighter: Latin America]] featherweight winner [[Yair Rodríguez]] and [[Alex Caceres]] served as the event headliner.{{cite web|url=http://www.flocombat.com/article/42543-ufc-targets-yair-rodriguez-vs-alex-caceres-for-aug-6-fs1-main-event|title=UFC targets Yair Rodriguez vs. Alex Caceres for Aug 6 FS1 Main Event |publisher=flocombat.com|author=Jeremy Botter|date=2016-06-09|access-date=2016-06-09}}{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/6/9/11897604/yair-rodriguez-vs-alex-caceres-in-the-works-for-ufc-event-in-utah|title=Yair Rodriguez vs. Alex Caceres in the works for UFC event in Utah|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2016-06-09|access-date=2016-06-09}}\n\n[[Brad Tavares]] was very briefly linked to a bout with [[Thales Leites]] at the event. However, Tavares was forced out of the fight with a rib injury and was replaced by [[Chris Camozzi]].{{cite web|url=http://sportv.globo.com/site/combate/noticia/2016/06/chris-camozzi-substitui-brad-tavares-e-e-o-novo-adversario-de-thales-leites.html|title=Chris Camozzi replaces Brad Tavares and is Thales Leites' new opponent (Brazilian Portuguese)|publisher=sportv.globo.com|author=Marcelo Barone and Raphael Marinho|date=2016-06-15|access-date=2016-06-15}}\n\n[[Justine Kish]] was expected to face [[Maryna Moroz]] at the event. However, Kish pulled out of the bout on July 22 due to a spiral fracture of her finger.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/07/justine-kish-out-at-ufc-fight-night-92-ufc-seeks-u-s-based-opponent-for-maryna-moroz|title=Justine Kish out at UFC Fight Night 92, UFC seeks U.S.-based opponent for Maryna Moroz|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-07-22|access-date=2016-07-22}} She was replaced six days later by promotion newcomer [[Danielle Taylor (fighter)|Danielle Taylor]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/07/newcomer-danielle-taylor-in-for-kish-vs-maryna-moroz-at-ufc-fight-night-92-in-utah|title=Newcomer Danielle Taylor in for Kish vs. Maryna Moroz at UFC Fight Night 92 in Utah|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-07-28|access-date=2016-07-28}}\n\nThis was the last UFC event to be promoted by [[Frank Fertitta III|Frank]] and [[Lorenzo Fertitta]].\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|[[Yair Rodríguez]]\n|def.\n|[[Alex Caceres]]\n|Decision (split) (46–49, 48–47, Canadian|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-08-27|access-date=2016-08-27}}\n|gate= {{Currency|995624|CAD}}\n|previousevent=[[UFC 202|UFC 202: Diaz vs. McGregor 2]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Arlovski vs. Barnett]]\n}}\n'''''UFC on Fox: Maia vs. Condit''''' (also known as '''''UFC on Fox 21''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on August 27, 2016, at [[Rogers Arena]] in [[Vancouver, British Columbia]], [[Canada]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-Returns-to-Vancouver-in-August-2016|title=UFC returns to Vancouver in August|publisher=ufc.com|author=UFC Press Release|date=2016-06-15|access-date=2016-06-15}}\n\n==Background==\nThis was the second time a [[UFC on Fox]] event was held outside the United States (the first one was [[UFC on Fox: Gustafsson vs. Johnson]], in [[Sweden]], in January 2015) and the first time in Canada.\n\nThe event was headlined by a welterweight contest between former [[List of World Extreme Cagefighting champions#Welterweight Championship|WEC Welterweight Champion]] and former interim [[UFC Welterweight Championship|UFC Welterweight Champion]] [[Carlos Condit]] and former [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] challenger [[Demian Maia]]. The bout was scheduled to take place at [[UFC 202]], but was moved a week later to headline this event.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/7/19/12231530/carlos-condit-vs-demian-maia-moved-from-ufc-202-to-ufc-on-fox-21-main|title=Carlos Condit vs. Demian Maia moved from UFC 202 to UFC on FOX 21 main event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-07-19|access-date=2016-07-19}}\n\n[[The Ultimate Fighter: China]] featherweight winner [[Ning Guangyou]] was expected to face [[Marlon Vera (fighter)|Marlon Vera]] at UFC 202. However, due to an out-of-competition failed test and following investigation concluding Ning ingested the substance without fault or negligence, the bout was moved to this event.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/8/9/12416676/ufc-s-ning-guangyou-fails-drug-test-for-clenbuterol-usada-finds-no|title=UFC's Ning Guangyou fails drug test for clenbuterol, USADA finds no fault|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-08-09|access-date=2016-08-09}} In turn, the bout was postponed again due to alleged [[visa (document)|visa]] issues for Ning, which restricted the timing of his travel.{{cite web|url=http://uk.ufc.com/news/Guangyou-Vera-off-vancouver|title=Guangyou-Vera bout off Vancouver card|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2016-08-22|access-date=2016-08-22}}\n\n[[Josh Emmett]] was expected to face promotional newcomer [[Jeremy Kennedy]] at the event. However, Emmett pulled out of the fight on August 19 with an undisclosed injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/08/josh-emmett-out-at-ufc-on-fox-21-promotion-seeks-canadian-lightweight-replacement|title=Josh Emmett out at UFC on FOX 21; promotion seeks Canadian lightweight replacement|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-08-19|access-date=2016-08-19}} He was replaced by fellow promotional newcomer Alessandro Ricci.{{cite web|url=http://mmasucka.com/2016/08/19/alex-ricci-steps-josh-emmett-jeremy-kennedy-ufc-fox-21/|title=Alex Ricci steps in for Josh Emmett against Jeremy Kennedy at UFC on FOX 21|publisher=mmasucka.com|author=Jeremy Brand|date=2016-08-19|access-date=2016-08-19}}\n\n[[The Ultimate Fighter Nations: Canada vs. Australia|The Ultimate Fighter Nations]] welterweight winner [[Chad Laprise]] missed weight, coming in at 159 lb. As a result, he was fined 20% of his fight purse, which went to his opponent [[Thibault Gouti]]. The fight proceeded as scheduled.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/08/ufc-on-fox-21-early-and-official-weigh-in-results-1|title=UFC on FOX 21 weigh-in results: Laprise heavy, but Condit (170.5) and Maia (171) on weight|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-08-26|access-date=2016-08-26}}\n\nA middleweight bout between promotional newcomers Adam Hunter and [[Ryan Janes (fighter)|Ryan Janes]] was canceled after the weigh-ins, due to a potential [[USADA]] (U.S. Anti-Doping Agency) violation stemming from a Hunter's pre-fight out-of-competition drug test. According to a UFC official, Janes was reportedly paid his \"show\" money.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/08/adam-hunter-pulled-from-ufc-on-fox-21-card-due-to-potential-usada-violation-fight-vs-janes-off|title=Adam Hunter pulled from UFC on FOX 21 card due to potential USADA violation, fight vs. Janes off|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2016-08-26|access-date=2016-08-27}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox) }}\n{{MMAevent '''''UFC Fight Night 94''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on September 17, 2016, at [[State Farm Arena (Hidalgo, Texas)|State Farm Arena]] in [[Hidalgo, Texas]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Hidalgo-Gets-UFC-Fight-Night-Card-on-Sept-17|title=Hidalgo gets UFC Fight Night card on Sept. 17 |publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2016-07-13|access-date=2016-07-13}}{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/07/ufc-heads-to-u-s-mexico-border-in-september-for-ufc-fight-night-94-in-hidalgo-texas|title=UFC heads to U.S.-Mexico border in September for UFC Fight Night 94 in Hidalgo, Texas |publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-07-13|access-date=2016-07-13}}\n\n==Background==\nAfter contesting several other [[Pay-per-view|PPV]] and Fight Night events across [[Texas]], this was the first that the promotion has hosted in [[Hidalgo, Texas|Hidalgo]].\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Dustin Poirier]] and [[Michael Johnson (fighter)|Michael Johnson]] served as the event headliner.{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Poirier-vs-Johnson-Headlines-in-Hidalgo|title=Poirier vs Johnson headlines in Hidalgo|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2016-07-18|access-date=2016-07-18}}\n\n[[Manvel Gamburyan]] was scheduled to face [[Alejandro Pérez (fighter)|Alejandro Pérez]] at the event. However, Gamburyan pulled out of the fight in mid-August for undisclosed personal reasons and was replaced by [[Albert Morales]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/08/albert-morales-replaces-manny-gamburyan-at-ufc-fight-night-94-in-texas|title=Albert Morales replaces Manny Gamburyan at UFC Fight Night 94 in Texas|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-08-17|access-date=2016-08-17}}\n\n[[Abel Trujillo]] was expected to face [[Evan Dunham]] at the event. However, Trujillo pulled out of the fight on September 5, citing an undisclosed injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/09/abel-trujillo-injured-and-out-of-ufc-fight-night-94-bout-with-evan-dunham|title=Abel Trujillo injured and out of UFC Fight Night 94 bout with Evan Dunham|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan and Dann Stupp|date=2016-09-05|access-date=2016-09-05}} In turn, former [[List of WSOF champions#Featherweight Championship|WSOF Featherweight Champion]] and promotional newcomer [[Rick Glenn]] was announced as his replacement.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/09/ex-wsof-champ-rick-glenn-signs-with-ufc-meets-evan-dunham-at-ufc-fight-night-94|title=With Trujillo out vs. Evan Dunham next week in Texas, UFC turning to a former WSOF champion|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Matt Erickson|date=2016-09-08|access-date=2016-09-08}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent Poirier vs. Johnson]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Lineker vs. Dodson]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Cyborg vs. Länsberg''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 95''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on September 24, 2016, at [[Nilson Nelson Gymnasium|Ginásio Nilson Nelson]] in [[Brasília]], [[Brazil]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-Returns-to-Brasilia-on-Sept-24|title=UFC returns to Brasilia on Sept. 24 |publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2016-07-30|access-date=2016-07-30}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event in [[Brasília]] was the second that the organization hosted in the [[Federal District (Brazil)|Brazilian capital]] city, with the first being [[UFC Fight Night: Bigfoot vs. Arlovski]] in September 2014.\n\nThe event was headlined by a catchweight bout of 140 lb between former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Women's Featherweight Championship|Strikeforce Women's Featherweight Champion]] and current [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Featherweight Championship|Invicta FC Featherweight Champion]] [[Cris Cyborg]] and promotional newcomer [[Lina Länsberg]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/cris-justino-cyborg-to-headline-september-24-2016-brasilia-event-vs-lina-lansberg|title=Cyborg next fight set; headlines Brasília event|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2016-08-04|access-date=2016-08-04}}{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/09/lineup-set-for-ufc-fight-night-95-including-six-fight-main-card-1|title=Lineup, including six-fight main card, set for UFC Fight Night 95 in Brasilia|author=Staff|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2016-09-06|access-date=2016-09-06}}\n\nOn August 13, the card suffered multiple changes due to injury: [[Brandon Thatch]] and [[Shinsho Anzai]] pulled out of their respective fights against [[Erick Silva]] and [[Luan Chagas]]. Silva and Chagas were instead rescheduled to face each other. [[Renato Moicano]] and [[Joaquim Silva (fighter)|Joaquim Silva]] also pulled out of their respective fights against [[Mike De La Torre]] and promotional newcomer [[Gregor Gillespie]]. Their replacements were [[Godofredo Pepey]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil 4]] winner Glaico França, respectively.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/08/injuries-force-several-changes-to-ufc-fight-night-95-in-brasilia|title=Injuries force several changes to UFC Fight Night 95 in Brasilia|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-08-13|access-date=2016-08-13}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Michel Prazeres]] missed weight by two pounds, weighing in at 158 lb. As a result, he was fined 20% of his fight purse, which went to [[Gilbert Burns]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/09/ufc-fight-night-95-early-weigh-in-results-10-a-m-et-1-1|title=UFC Fight Night 95 official weigh-in results: 'Cyborg' uses the extra pound, but headliner set|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-09-23|access-date=2016-09-23}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Catchweight (140
